1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/Ar+yA tsa la kalpa tan+t+ra rA dza/bod skad du/'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po/dpal ngag gi dbang phyug la phyag 'tshal lo//
1b2(rgyud): 'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/de nas yang de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen pos/bdud thams cad zil gyis gnon pa/rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la 
1b3(rgyud): snyoms par zhugs nas nyid kyi sku dang /gsung dang /thugs rdo rje las sngags kyi rgyal po 'di phyung ngo /oM AHhUM/de gsung ma thag tu tshangs pa dang /khyab 'jug dang /dbang phyug chen 
1b4(rgyud): po dang /brgya byin dang /gzhon nu dang /snying gi ri ti dang /tshogs kyi bdag po dang /b+hai ba sa ta dang /dga' byed dbang phyug dang /nag po chen po dang /srin mo chen mo 'phro 
1b5(rgyud): byed ma dang /nyi ma dang /zla ba dang /mig dmar dang /lag pa dang /phur bu dang /spen pa dang /pa ba sangs dang /sgra gcan dang /rgyu skar nyi shu rtsa brgyad dang /gnod sbyin 
2a1(rgyud): @#//dang /srin po dang /chu lha dang /gshin rje dang /me lha dang /rlung gi lha dang /sa'i lha mo dang /dga' bo dang /nye dga' bo dang /'jog po dang /mtha' yas dang /dung skyong dang /rigs ldan dang /pad+ma dang /pad+ma chen po dang /
2a2(rgyud): srin po'i bdag po la ngag mgrin bcu dang /lha'i bu drug khri la sogs pas steng dang /sa bla dang /sa 'og pa thams cad skad cig de nyid la dpal 'byung ba'i mchod rten gyi spo de la 'khod do//de'i tshe 'jig rten gsum gyi bdud thams cad der lhags 
2a3(rgyud): nas byang chub chen po'i bar du gcod par rtsom mo//de rnams kyis thal mo sbyar te mgrin gcig tu 'di skad ces gsol to//kye bcom ldan 'das ston pa bde bar gshegs pa byang chub kyi bar du gcod pa'i bdud kyi rigs 'di rnams zhi ba dang med 
2a4(rgyud): par mdzad du gsol zhes pa dang /de nas bcom ldan 'das bdud thams cad las mchog tu rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas gsang sngags 'di nyid sku dang /gsung dang /thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo //hUM hUM hUM zhes brjod ma thag tu 
2a5(rgyud): nyid kyi mdun du khro bo chen po mi g.yo ba sku mdog mthing nag tu 'dug pa/zhal stong phrag stong ha har bzhad pa/zhabs stong phrag stong brkyang bskum gyi tshul du bzhugs pa/phyag stong phrag stong du gdug pa'i mtshon cha ral gri dang /ral gri kyog po dang /chu 
2a6(rgyud): gri dang /gri gug dang /spu gri dang /kha T+wAM ga dang /rtse gsum dang /rtse dgu pa dang /mdung dang /'phen mdung dang /ldong mdung dang /mda' dang /mda' ste'u kha dang /mda' rtse lnga pa dang /mda' kha brag pa dang /rdo rje rtse gcig pa dang /rtse gsum pa dang /
2b1(rgyud): rtse dgu pa dang /rtse mo bcu gsum pa dang /sta re dang /dgra sta dang /sta leb dang /so sta dang /'phen sta dang /bzhar sta dang /dbyug pa dang /tho ba dang /gtun shing dang /'khor lo dang /phub dang /mgo bo dang /ping ti pa la dang /gzhu dang /gzhu mnyen 
2b2(rgyud): dang /thod pa dang /thod tshal dang /gri zlum dang /gri rings dang /gri lcags kyo dang /zhags pa dang /rnga yab dang /ba dan dang /me'i phung po dang /chu'i skyogs dang /brag dang /thod pa khrag gis gang ba dang /phur ba dang /thod pa'i phreng ba dang /zhing dbyug 
2b3(rgyud): dang /me ri dang /so'i phreng ba dang /dril bu dang /jeng dang /pi wang dang /gling bu dang /rdza rnga dang /pa Ta ha dang /rnga zlum dang /rked nyag dang /dung dang /bsil phreng dang /lag pa dang /rkang ba dang /khab dang /gsil byed dang /nam mkha' lding dang /sbrul zhags 
2b4(rgyud): la sogs pa'i mtshon cha du ma bzung nas phyag lhag ma rnams g.yas na ral gri phyar nas g.yon pa sdigs mdzub phyar bar mdzad do//zhal stong phrag stong la 'dzum pa dang /bzhad pa dang /ha ha zhes sgrogs pa dang /mche ba gtsigs pa dang /so 'dar ba dang /gyen du 
2b5(rgyud): gdangs pa dang /khrag bldag pa dang /rngam pa dang /zhi bar yang mdzad de/zhal gyi kha dog kyang tha dad do//dbu skra yang nag po dang /ser po dang /dmar po la sogs pa tha dad do//sku stong la glang po che'i ko ba gyon nas/sku smad kyang stag gi pags pas dkris 
2b6(rgyud): shing /phyag dang /zhal dang /zhabs kyi bye brag gis rgyan yang tha dad de/rin po che du ma dang /rus pa'i phreng ba dang /sbrul gyi chun po dang /mi mgo'i phreng ba dang /mi'i pags pa dang /seng ge'i pags pa dang /ma he'i pags pa la sogs pa tha dad do/zhal re re 
2b7(rgyud): la yang spyan gsum gsum dang /gnyis gnyis dang /re re'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis khro bo chen po mi g.yo ba de la 'di skad du bka' bsgo'o//oM kye mi g.yo ba/khro bo drag po/'jigs byed chen po/gtum po chen po/dpa' bo chen po/bdud kyi 
3a1(rgyud): @#//tshogs thams cad sod cig/chings shig/btogs shig/rgyob cig/phrol cig/gtubs shig/dril cig/phul cig/non cig/gzir cig/phug cig/tshos shig/sregs shig/phye mar gyis shig/med par gyis shig/
3a2(rgyud): khos shig/sdoms shig/zo shig/'thur shig/sdigs shig/hUM hUM hUM dza dza haM laM traM phaT/ces gsungs pa dang /khro bo'i rgyal po de khros nas hUM zhes brjod pas zhal gsum phyag drug par byas te/klu'i tshogs thams cad ma lus par zos te/phaT ces 
3a3(rgyud): brjod nas zhal bzhi phyag bzhi zhabs kyang bzhir byas nas lha ma yin thams cad med par byas so//zhal gcig phyag gnyis pa drang srong khros pa'i dbyibs ltar byas nas lha'i gdon thams cad pham par byas te/tshogs kyi bdag po gdan du byas so//gzhon nu'i 
3a4(rgyud): cha byad du byas nas pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs nas lha'i bu'i bdud pham par byas so//g.yon pa sa la btsugs nas bi nA ya ka med par byas so//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//lha la sogs pa rnams sdang zhing //khros nas gdug pa rnams 'jigs byed//
3a5(rgyud): sna tshogs rab mang sbyor mdzad pa'o//chos rnams gang gis cir ma mthong //lus dang sems dang ngag rnams dag/kun rdzob stong pa rnam dag nas//mi gnas sna tshogs zung 'jug bde//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las sprul pa rnam par 
3a6(rgyud): dag pa'i le'u ste dang po'o////de nas yang dag bshad pa ba//bde bar gshegs rnams 'dus pa'i sku//sna tshogs lus can 'jigs pa'i gzugs//nam mkha' gang ba'i bsam gtan bya//skad cig bsam gtan mngon rdzogs shing //skad 
3a7(rgyud): cig yi ge rnam dgod byed//skad cig la ni gsang sngags 'don//'jigs gzugs skad cig sprul pa 'gyed//tshar gcod zil gyis gnon mdzad phyir//skad cig khros spyan nag po 'bar//mkha' la gzigs pas lha rnams skrag/sa la gzigs pas klu rnams 
3b1(rgyud): skrag/rgyal ba sras dang bcas rnams dang //bla ma la yang phyag 'tshal zhing //rtag tu mchod de dga' chen bskyed//lus ngag yid kyi sdig bral gang //dkon mchog gsum mchod bde don gnyer//sangs rgyas bzhugs shing chos gsungs pa'i//smon lam dag pa'i 
3b2(rgyud): blo bskyed bya//byang chub sems kyang bskyed bya zhing //dge ba'i phung po dus gsum du//gzhan gyi don du bsngo bar bya//byams dang snying rje bsam par bya//dga' ba'i dge la yid kyis dmigs//nam mkha' che mtshungs blo yis ni//btang snyoms chen po 
3b3(rgyud): bsgom par bya//de ltar dang ldan brtson 'grus can//g.yo sgyu bral zhing bla ma bkur//snying brtse le lo med pa yi//srid pa'i sred pa spong byed pa//dga' ba'i tshal lam ne'u gsing dang //phug gam lha khang stong pa'am//shing drung nags dgon ljongs 
3b4(rgyud): rnams su//bde ba'i yid kyis gnas par bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen po yongs su rdzogs pa thams cad mkhyen pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas rig pa 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo //nam mkha'i 
3b5(rgyud): dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//hU~M gi yi ge las skyes pa//mi g.yo mgon po rgyal po'i skur//rnal 'byor dag pa'i lus bsgom bya//zhal gcig phyag gnyis brjid pa'i sku//lha dang lha min bdud tshogs rnams//shin tu skrag par mdzad pa'i phyir//g.yas kyis 
3b6(rgyud): ral gri mkha' la phyar//g.yon pa sdigs mdzub zhags pa 'dzin//pad+ma nyi ma'i gdan la ni//tshogs kyi bdag po'i steng gnas pa//g.yon brkyang gar bzhugs stag sham can//phuHsgrogs klu chen brgyad kyis brgyan//dmar ser gyen sgra spyan rtsa dmar//spyan zlum 
3b7(rgyud): khro tshul mche ba gtsigs//tshangs pa lha dbang khyab 'jug dang //dbang phyug che sogs lha rnams dang //lha ma yin rnams skrag par byed//bskal pa'i me 'od 'bar dbus na//khro bo du ma'i tshogs dang bcas//brtan g.yo khams gsum ma lus rnams//rang gi 
4a1(rgyud): @#//sprul pa shes byas nas//de ltar gus bcas yun rings su//rgyun mi 'chad par gang bsgoms pas//las dang nyon mongs kyis bslad pa'i//tha mal pa yi rnam rtog ldog/bdag 'dod lha yi rnal 'byor la//shin tu yid brtan byed gyur na//ral gri 
4a2(rgyud): la sogs grub brgyad kyi//snod du rnal 'byor pa de 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen pos mi bzad pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas rig pa 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus 
4a3(rgyud): gnas par//haM gi yi ge las skyes par//drag chen khro bo'i rgyal po ni//sngon gzhal gsum phyag drug bsgom//ral gri chu gri sta re ni//g.yas kyi phyag na rim bzhin 'dzin//sdigs mdzub 'khor lo kha T+wAM ni//g.yon gyi phyag na rim bzhin 'dzin//spyan gsum khro gnyer 
4a4(rgyud): gyen du bskyod//dbu skra smar gyen 'greng ser//rol pa'i klu yi rgyan gyis brgyan//thod pa'i phreng ba stag sham can//glang chen pags pas stod g.yogs byas//rus pa'i rgyan gyis rab brgyan cing //pad+ma nyi ma'i gdan dbus na//g.yon brkyang g.yas bskum 
4a5(rgyud): tshul du 'gying //he pheM nam mkha'i phyogs sgrogs pa//bskal pa'i me ltar gzi brjid che//'di ltar skad cig dran pa'am//ming shes tsam gyis 'dod pa sgrub//de nas bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen po 'jig rten dbang phyug rnam par dag pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la 
4a6(rgyud): snyoms par zhugs so//rig pa 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//ma~M las skyes pa'i mi g.yo ba//'jigs byed chen po bsam par bya//ya~M dag sa drod kyi steng na 'gying //zhal bzhi phyag bzhi zhabs 
4a7(rgyud): kyang bzhi//g.yas pa ral gri rdo rje 'dzin//g.yon pa sdigs mdzub thod khrag go//zhabs bzhi bdud bzhi'i steng na 'gying //sna tshogs rus pa'i rgyan ldan zhing //stag gi sham thabs rnam par brgyan//stod du na ra'i pags pas brgyan//dmar 
4b1(rgyud): ser gyen skra 'jigs tshul che//ha har bzhad cing rta skad 'tsher//khro bo du ma'i sprin spro zhing //zhal rnams gdengs pas mche ba gtsigs//spyan bcu gnyis po rab tu bgrad//'jigs pa yid ni 'jigs par byed//char sprin mdog 'dra me chen gzi//thabs chen 
4b2(rgyud): 'dod pa'ang 'dis rdzogs byed//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen po mi 'gyur rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas rig pa 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo //nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//yi ge hUM las byung 
4b3(rgyud): ba yi//mi g.yo mgon po gtum po che//bdud rnams skrag byed bsgom par bya//zhal gcig phyag gnyis 'jigs pa'i sku//pad dkar nyi ma'i steng dkyil na//pus mo sa la btsugs nas bzhugs//phyag g.yas ral gri 'dzin pa ste//g.yon pa sdigs mdzub 
4b4(rgyud): zhags pa 'dzin//spyan gnyis dmar la zlum pa ste//gzhon nu khros pa'i cha byad can//rin chen rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//sbrul dkar po yis do shal byas//stag gi pags pa'i sham thabs can//sna tshogs khro bo rnam par 'phro//bskal pa'i me yi dbus 
4b5(rgyud): na gnas//gang gis nyi ma zla ba yi//mdun du rnam par dran byed na//bdud rnams ma lus zad nas ni//bde ba chen po'i lus rnyed 'gyur//a ta sa yi me tog gi/'od ldan bcom ldan lha yis mchod//bdag gi snying ngam snying dkyil bsgom//myur 
4b6(rgyud): du 'dod pa rdzogs par byed//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//zhal gcig phyag gnyis du ba'i mdog/yang na sngon po baiDUr gzi//bdud rnams brlag par mdzad pa'o//g.yon brkyang g.yas bskum bzhugs pa'am//yang na g.yon pas 
4b7(rgyud): la btsugs//thabs tshul rin chen rgyan gyis brgyan//sdig pa thams cad zad byed pa//blo gros phung po rgyas par byed//de nas bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen po yongs su rgyas pa dpal gyi rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas rig pa 'di nyid kyi 
5a1(rgyud): @#//sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//dam tshig sems dpa' lta bu yi//ye shes sems dpa' snying gar bsgom//'od ni cher 'bar drag po'i gzi//shes bya'i sgrib las rnam par rgyal//de yi thugs kar mtshan ma ste//sa bon rtag 
5a2(rgyud): par dmigs pa ni//las kyi sgrib pa rnam dag rgyu//las kyi las rnams bsgrub pa'i phyir//sa bon las ni 'od zer 'phros//ye shes sems dpa' bkug nas gzhug/des ni mi shes bag chags rnams//blo ldan blo la 'jog mi byed//de bzhin gshegs 
5a3(rgyud): pa kun dmigs nas//sangs rgyas rnams kyis dbang bskur 'gyur//spyi bor rang gi rigs bsams nas//rigs kyi bdag pos dbu brgyan 'gyur//de ltar de la brtson chen bya//'dis ni rgyal rnams mnyes gyur nas//myur du rgyal ba nyid thob 'gyur//'phags 
5a4(rgyud): pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las lha 'byung ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////
5b1(rgyud): mdzub dang /'khor lo dang /kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa dang /klus brgyan cing thod pa'i do shal can/stag gi sham thabs dang /glang po che'i stod g.yogs dang /thod pa lnga'i dbu rgyan can/rus pas brgyan cing pad+ma dang /nyi ma'i steng nag yon brkyang zhing g.yas bskum pa'i tshul 
5b2(rgyud): gyis 'gying ba/bskal pa'i me'i gzi mdangs kyi nang nas khro bo bcu 'phro ba bri'o//lha min rgyal po mi g.yo ba ni zhal bzhi re re la yang spyan gsum gsum dang ldan zhing /phyag bzhi'i g.yas gnyis na ral gri dang po/sngon gnyis na sdigs mdzub dang thod khrag can/
5b3(rgyud): stod la mi'i pags pa gyon zhing stag gi sham thabs can/rus pa dang /klus yan lag brgyan pa/bskal pa'i me 'bar zhing khro bo'i tshogs kyi sprin du ma 'phro ba/pad+ma la zhabs bzhis bdud bzhi mnan cing skra ser gyon du brdzes pa bri'o//bdud thams cad 'dul 
5b4(rgyud): ba'i mi g.yo ba ni gtum po zhal gcig phyag gnyis ral gri dang /zhags pas 'brel ba/skra nag po 'am ser po gyen du brdzes pa/rin po che'i cod pan dang /do shal dang /yan lag thams cad brgyan cing stag gi sham thabs can /sbrul dkar po se ral khar 
5b5(rgyud): thogs pa/bskal pa'i tshogs kyi mer 'bar ba'i nang na khro bo du ma 'khrug cing /pad+ma dang nyi ma la pus mo g.yas pa 'thab stangs kyi tshul du bzhugs pa 'am/yang na g.yon pa gshed po'i tshul du btsugs shing lhag ma nam mkha' la 'geng zhing bteg pa bri'o//
5b6(rgyud): gang yang rang 'dod pa zhig de ltar bris nas/de'i mdun du rang nyid kyi 'dra 'bag tshon sna tshogs kyis bris la/ri mo mkhan la go ba legs par bskyed nas/mdzes pa g.yon du phul te/bkra shis brjod nas/rang gi gnas kyi dben pa'i phyogs su bzhugs pa mngon sum 
5b7(rgyud): du mi g.yo bar bsgrubs te/me tog la sogs pas legs par mchod nas/ye shes kyi khro bo'i rgyal po yang de nyid la bstim par bya'o//gang yang dngos grub 'dod pas spos dang /me tog dang /sgron ma dang /zhal zas dang /gos la sogs pas rgyun du 
6a1(rgyud): @#//mchod na sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang sngags 'grub pa la gdon mi za'o//'dir skye bo 'ga' ni dman pa ste/skur pa 'debs na shin tu yang lhung bar 'gyur ba'i phyir sku dben pa'i sa phyogs su sba bar bya'o//zhi rgyas dbang dang bsad pa 'am//
6a2(rgyud): ral gri la sogs brgyad rnams dang //mchog gi sangs rgyas thams cad ni//rgyal ba'i gzugs brnyan las rnyed 'gyur//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po las bris sku'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//gsang ba rnam par 
6a3(rgyud): dag byed cing //dngos grub myur phyir gsang sngags rnams//rim pa dbye ba ngo mtshar nyon//bcu bzhi'i dbyangs las yum gyis brgyan//gsal byed gnyis pa'i dang po la//gsum pa'i lnga pas nye bar brgyan//gsum pa'i gsum pa der bzhag go//lnga pa'i lnga pa brgyad pa la//
6a4(rgyud): dbyangs kyi gnyis pas mdzes par byas//me dang dbyangs bcu gsum par 'brel//drod gnyis pa dang drag mtha' 'brel//sum cu gsum dang dbyangs drug 'brel//nyi shu rtsa lnga pas brgyan no//nyi shu gnyis dang bcu gcig 'brel//de la nyi zlas rnam brgyan pa'o//
6a5(rgyud): gsang sngags rgyal po yi ge bcu//stong khri 'bum mam bye ba bcu//sa ya bzlas kyang lha mthong 'gyur//snying po sdig bral dga' ba ste//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kun gyis bsngags//sdug bsngal zad byed bde ba che//sgrub pa rnams la skyed byed pa'i//
6a6(rgyud): gsang sngags drag po dran byed na//'jig rten 'dir yang tshe ring 'gyur//sangs rgyas 'gyur ro gzhan ci smos//oM swa b+hA ba shud d+hAHsarba d+harmAHswA b+hA ba shud+d+ho&haM/gzung ba dag pa ye shes kyi sngags so//oM shU n+ya tA dz+nyA na badzra swa b+hA wa At+ma ko&haM/'dzin pa'i sems dag pa 
6a7(rgyud): ye shes kyi sngags so//o badzra shud d+hAHsarba d+harmA badzra shud+d+ho&haM/sku brtan pa rdo rje ye shes kyi sngags so/oM kA ya bA kyi tA badzra swa b+hA ba At+ma ko&haM/sku gsung thugs kyi ye shes kyi sngags so//aM a tsa la te dza badzra puSh+Ti/d+hU pe/dI pe/gan d+he/nai bi d+yes pa ri wA re b+h+yaHdzaHhUM baM ho//
6b1(rgyud): gan+d+hAM b+hA sa rba sa twA nAM/i maM ba liM bri s+ya ga ga bA hi bA hi b+ha k+Sha b+ha k+Sha Ni hU~M phaT/mchod pa dang gtor ma'i sngags so//zho dang 'o ma 'bras chan dang //sA lu dkar dang ti la dkar dang //gro dang nas dang bra bo dang //shing tog la sogs rtag par mchod//oM tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na hU~M maN+Da 
6b2(rgyud): lam d+h+ya A gats+tsha ya/sa ma ya staM/spyan drang ba'i sngags so//oM tsaN+Da ma hA ra Sha Na ma ma sha truM a mu ka s+ya sha rI raM kI la ya hU~M phaT/ka ti mu ta stam b+ha ya/hrI di kI la ya hU~M phaT /pA de kI la ya swA hA/phur bus gdab pa'i sngags so//na maHsa man+ta kA ya wA k+tsit+ta badzrA 
6b3(rgyud): NAM/oM a tsa la kA na tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na s+pho Ta ya hU~M trA~M ma~M hoM/las la bskul ba'i sngags so//na maHsaman+ta kA ya wA k+tsi t+ta badzrA NAM/oM a tsa la ka na tsaN+Da ta na ta na/maT+Ta maT+Ta/mo Ta mo Ta/taT+Ta taT+Ta/ha na ha na/da ha da ha/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/A be sha ya A be sha ya/ma hA man+ta ba la ka/
6b4(rgyud): d+hu na d+hu na/ti ni ti ni/kha da kha da/big+h+nAn/ma ra mar duSh+TAn duSh+TAn ra kA haM/sa d+hAn/ku ru ku ru/ki ri ki ri/ma hA big+h+nA badzra/s+pho Ta ya s+pho Ta ya/hU~M hU~M hU~M/tri ba let+ta raM ga na rak+ta/oM oM oM/hU~M hU~M hU~M /a tsa la ta dza/s+pho Ta ya s+pho Ta ya /hU~M hU~M/a man+ti ka/
6b5(rgyud): kro d+ha ma hA ba la/shro d+hA ya/ma nA ya/man+ta ha~M maM/shud+d+ha tu mo k+Sha/du pya du/badzra nA ma taM/a pra ti ta te b+h+yaH/dzA hI ya trA ya/a sa ha na/ma hA swA hA/las thams cad kyi rjes su 'gro ba'i sngags so//na maHsaman+ta kA ya wA k+tsit+ta badzrA NAM/oM tra tra a mo g+ha/
6b6(rgyud): tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na/s+pho Ta ya hU~M phaT/tra ma ya tra ma ya hU~M/tra ta haM ma/oM ba len+ta te dzo mA li ni swA hA/cho ga thams cad kyi rjes su 'gros ba'i sngags so//na maHsa man+ta kA ya wA k+tsi t+ta badzrA NAM/oM a mo g+ha e h+ye hi a pra ti ha ta e h+ye hi a tsa la wa te dza/a nan+ta ka na/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/tsu ru tsu 
6b7(rgyud): ru/ta Ti ta Ti/ta Ta ta Ta/big+h+nAM b+hA ta ya/mA ra ya/khA da ya/s+pho Ta ya/tra ta staM/tsa men/a nu ban+d+ha/A dzA ku ru/a sa ha da/a na a gra/ka Ta ka Ta/ma Ta ma Ta/haM haM/a Ti ba la/e h+ye hi ma hA kA ru Na/bi Sha Na/nan+ta nan+ta/A be sha ya A be sha ya/
7a1(rgyud): @#//a tsa la te dza/sarba tra a nut+ta re ki tsa ta/ta Ta ta Ta/d+harma d+harma/phaT phaT te/'di ni bsrung ba dam pa'i sngags so//khro bo'i rgyal po mi g.yo ba'i//gang gis gsang sngags rnams shes na//yi ge tsam gyis bdud brlag byed//mang du brjod na 
7a2(rgyud): smos ci dgos//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las gsang sngags rnams 'byung ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//gsang sngags grub pa las byung ba'i//las kyi sbyor ba'i dbye ba yis//'dod rnams 
7a3(rgyud): shin tu 'grub byed de//bsam gtan rnam dag chos rnam dag/ye shes rnam dag sku rnam dag/rang byung rnam dag don rnam dag/bya ba rnam dag byed pa dag/sgyu ma sgyu ma'i ye shes che//sgyu 'phrul lta bu'i 'khrul 'khor dang //mig yor smig rgyu'i gzugs 
7a4(rgyud): brnyan 'brel//sna tshogs chu bur bzhin g.yo ba'i//gang gis bdud las rgyal byed pa'i//yungs kar rgyal po bsgrub par bya//rgyal gyi skar mar btsugs byas te//thod pa dkar po dri chus bran//'di ni las rnams kun bya'i phyir//dus su bsrung zhing 
7a5(rgyud): bskyed bya ste//dus su mchod dang gtor ma byas//bar chad kun las rnam rgyal yin//yang na gro ga'am spra tshil la sprul nag po'i gzugs byas nas gu gul tshil chen gyis bdug cing sngags drag tu brjod de/gang 'dod pa'i lus la byabs nas/khrus byas te rtswa 
7a6(rgyud): ku sha'i chun pos 'og nas g.yug cing /nA ga rA dza sa ma ya dun ti/phu Ta phuT phaT ces brjod na/gang la klu gnod pa de sos bar byed do//yang na gong du bstan pa'i yungs kar dang /gu gul gyis bdug cing sngags dragatu brjod na klu 'bros so//slar yang 
7a7(rgyud): mi ldog go//'byung pa la sogs pa'i gdon gyis zin pa la yungs kar bskyed pa la sngags mang du bzlas nas gtor na 'byer ro//byis pa gsar du btsas pa bsrung ba ni kham phor la rdo rgyus kyis bris pa bcad do//sgra tshil gyi gzugs sor bzhi pa'i tshad du byas pa'i 
7b1(rgyud): snying gar bsgrub bya'i ming dang bcas pa'i sngags bcug la khal tsher ma btsugs na bsgrub bya'i kha la tsher ma btsugs par 'gyur ro//rkang pa la btsugs na rengs so//snying la gtsugs na dran pa stor bar byed do//mi'i rdzing ra gyi rus pa'i phur bu'am/lcags phur 
7b2(rgyud): yan lag gang dang gang la btab pa de zha ba'am rengs par byed do//dur khrod kyi thal ba la mang du bsngags nas dgra bo'i sgo 'og tu btab na 'byer ro//g.yul du zhugs nas ral gri la bsngags te/bzung na rgyal lo//gang la yang gtor ma'i bye brag gis 'grub 
7b3(rgyud): nges so//dur khrod du phyin te snod thod pa la pad snal dkar po'i sdong bu dang /tshil chen gyi mar me byas la tshes brgyad dam bcu bzhi la sbar ba'i du ba mig la byugs na gzhan gyis mthong na dga' bar 'gyur ro//gal te nyi ma'am zla ba gzas bzung ba'i tshe mi g.yo mgon 
7b4(rgyud): po'i sku kham pa la byas nas me tog shel pa li ka 'am/daN+Do N+pa las mchod cing sngags mtshan thog thag bzlas pas tshe lo stong thub po//yang zla ba gzas bzung ba'i tshes dum bu drug pa'i thod par khrag sna tshogs blugs te/maN+Dal nag po'i thog tu bzhag nas kha me yong 
7b5(rgyud): gis bcad de/de'i steng du kham pa las byas pa'i mi g.yo ba bzhag ste/rang nyid gcer bu'i lus ma he'i rnam lngas byugs te/mtshan thog thag bzlas na srid pa gsum mthong bar 'gyur ro//yang nyi ma dang zla ba gzas bzung ba'i cho maN+Dal gru bzhi pa byas nas grwa 
7b6(rgyud): bzhir ral gri btsugs la/rang nyid gcer bu lus thams cad thal bas byugs te mtshan thog thag bzlas na sred pa gsum gsal bar 'gyur ro//gal te gter kha dbye bar 'dod pas mi g.yo mgon po'i sku kham pa las byas la/sman drug la sogs pa dri zhim po 
7b7(rgyud): thams cad dang lhan cig pa'i rin po che'i snod kha sbyar du bcug nas maN+Dal dkar po'i dbus su khri'u la bzhag nas/dus gsum du mchod cing bskor ba brgya rtsa brgyad byas te/gsol pa gdab cing a ka ru la sogs pa'i spos sreg cing bstod pa byas na zla ba gsum 
8a1(rgyud): @#//gyi rjes su gter rnyed nges so//gal te char 'bebs par 'dod na/klu yod pa'i gnas su klu'i dkyil 'khor byas te/spra tshil lam bag zan la dga' bo la sogs pa'i gzugs byas te/kha dog tha dad par byas nas maN+Dal der gzhug ste/dam tshig pa 
8a2(rgyud): bskyed nas dbus su mi g.yo mgon po dang bcas pa la ye shes kyi lha bcu gnas/ka ra dang /bu ram dang /sbrang dang /'o ma la sogs pas mchod cing rtsa ba'i sngags 'brug ltar bsngags pas klu rnams skrag nas char pa'i rgyun 'bebs par byed do//gal te mi 'beb 
8a3(rgyud): na thod pa dum bu gcig par klu'i gzugs rnams bcug la/gdug pa can gyi mes bsros na char shing rta'i 'phang lo tsam zhag bdun du 'bebs pa la the tshom med do//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las sbyor ka phra mo'i le'u ste lnga pa'o////de nas yang dag 
8a4(rgyud): bshad bya ba//bsam gtan brtan las byung ba ni//'khrul med nyid de gnod rnams sel//khams gsum rgyal po cher nyon cig/spro bsdu'i rim pa goms gyur las//ji srid 'dod pa'i dus bar du//rang sems gnas nas brtan par 'gyur//dngos med dngos 
8a5(rgyud): sogs ma lus pa'i//rnam rtog rab rib kun bsal nas//nyon mongs nad rnams ma lus pa//bag chags dang bcas gtan bcom nas//'phral du yang ni brod dga' skye//spro yang mi dmigs bsdu mi dmigs//skye med 'gag med ting nge 'dzin//'byung 
8a6(rgyud): med 'gro med bsam gtan la//gnas na ye shes myur du thob//bla med ye shes yon tan dang //mthu rnams che bar 'gyur ba ni//bla ma mnyes las rnyed 'gyur ba'i//de nyid bdud rtsi bcud kyis len//gus pas rtag par nyin mtshan du//rgyun chad med par 
8a7(rgyud): goms byas na//myur du phyag rgya chen po yi//dngos grub snod du 'gyur ba yin//'bad rtsol byang chub yan lag ste//le lo gnyid dang g.yeng ba spang //sngags gsal dmigs pa brtan gyur cing //bsam gtan thon nas 'khrul 'khor brtsam//skar ma rgyal gyi 
8b1(rgyud): nyin par la//gro ga 'am yang na shin shun la//gur gum gi wang tsan dan gyis//rdo rje rgya gram mu khyud bcas//dbus su ming dang sngags spel bri//mu khyud la ni rtsa sngags bri//rwa kun la ni hU~M dang ming //spel te g.yas nas bskor la bri//dkyil 'khor dkar 
8b2(rgyud): po'i dbus su ni//me tog dkar po'i steng bzhag nas//lha yi skur ni bskyed byas te//stong rtsa brgyad du bzlas byas nas//gang zhig 'dod pa'i dpung pa'am//lus la bcangs na srung bar byed//kham phor sar pa la rdo rgyus kyis bris te/sgor bcangs na gsar 
8b3(rgyud): du btsas pa'i byis pa srung ngo //dur khrod kyi ras la sngags ming dang bcas pa bris te/spra tshil gyis btums nas dur khrod kyi bum par bcug nas/kha rdo rje rgya gram gyis bcad de sbas na mngal skems par byed do//phyung nas 'o mas bkrus na sos so//gal te bsad 
8b4(rgyud): par 'dod na dkyil 'khor gru gsum nag por byas pa'i steng du kham pa las byas pa'i mi g.yo mgon po zhal gcig phyag bzhi pa/g.yas pa gnyis na ral gri dang /rdo rje sdigs mdzub dang thod khrag g.yon na 'chang ba byas pa/rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa bzhugs 
8b5(rgyud): so//de nas dri ma drug dang bag zan las bsgrub bya'i gzugs sor brgyad byas nas/rdo rje rgya gram gyi 'khor lo bsad pa'i sham bu dang bcas pa snying gar bcug ste/de'i sku'i zhabs su bzhag nas rang nyid lha'i ting nge 'dzin dang ldan pas zur bzhir mi rus sam seng ldeng ngam/
8b6(rgyud): lcags las byas pa'i phur bu sor bzhi pa bzhag pa la thun mtshams thams cad du btab pas shes pa brgyal bar bsam mo//de'i 'od kyis mdun gyi phur pa mi g.yo bar bsgoms pa'i thugs bskul nas/de las khro bo lcags kyu dang zhags pa thogs pa grangs med pa spros 
8b7(rgyud): pas/bsgrub bya rlung gi 'khor lo la bzhag nas 'ongs pas skad cig tsam gyis phung po gtor nas/shes pa ling ga la bstim par bya'o//da ltar zhag nyi shu rtsa gcig tu byas pas hom khung du bsreg pa'am/mnan pa byas na dbang phyug chen po yang gsod par des na gzhan lta 
9a1(rgyud): @#//smos kyang ci dgos/yang na d+ha du ra dang /khyi khrag dang /nya'i khrag gis 'khor lo ral gri'i rtse mo ltar rno bar byas nas/lte ba'i dbus su gru gsum ma'i nang du bsgrub bya'i ming dang rgyud du bcas pa bris la/de zhags pas bskor bar bya'o//rtsibs 
9a2(rgyud): rnams su sngags kyi rkang pa brgyad pa bri'o//mu khyud la ming dang bcas pa'i snying po bri'o//de'i rgyab kyi mu khyud la las thams cad pa bri'o//mu khyud gsum pa la rdo rje dang ral gri spel nas bri bar bya'o//de nas thod pa kha sbyar gyi nang du bcug nas zhing sgras bcings 
9a3(rgyud): te|bsgrub bya dag za ba'i bsam gtan dang ldan pas khro bo shin tu khros pas khrag 'dren ba'i lta bas bltas na zhag nyi shu rtsa gcig gis gang la byas pa de khrag tu sgyugs nas 'chi'o//dur khrod du sbas na mtshon gyi bskal pa babs nas 'chi'o//shing gcig gi rtsar 
9a4(rgyud): sbas na rengs so//chu mdor sbas na 'byer ro//lam rgya gram du sbas na smyo'o//lha rten zhabs su sbas na zha bor 'gyur ro//klu yod pa'i sar sbas na mdzes zin pa la the tshom med do//rang nyid kyi nyal ba'i zhabs su sbas na 'dod pa'i bu mo khugs so//ci ste rgyal 
9a5(rgyud): mtshan gyi rtse mo la btags na dmag kyang rengs so//de dag thams cad la khro bo 'bar ba dud pa'i kha dog dang ldan pa phyag bzhi zhabs bzhi spyan gsum dang ldan par blta'o//dur khrod kyi ras la dmag dpon gyi ming dang spel ba'i sngags rdo rje rgya gram gyi lte bar bris la 
9a6(rgyud): mtha' yi ge laM brgyad kyis mnan te/de'i phyi rol du dbang chen gyi dkyil 'khor la rdo rje rgya gram bris la/de sbra tshil las byas pa'i tshogs kyi bdag po'i snying gar bcug nas gang 'thab pa'i mtshams su bzhag na dmag rengs so//gzhan yang gza' skar la bltas te 'phyar ro//
9a7(rgyud): bskrad par 'dod na gro ga'am ras la bya rog dang 'ug pa'i khrag gis gong du gsungs pa'i 'khor lo bris te/uts+tsha Ta ya spel bar bya'o//char che ba'am ser ba gcad par 'dod na/gro ga la gur gum mam gi wang gis 'khor lo rtsibs bzhi pa byas la mu khyud gsum pa byas te/
9b1(rgyud): rtsibs bzhi la snying po'i sngags yi ge hU~M la bskor ba bris la/dbus kyi lte bar yi ge k+ShiM bris te/de la rtsa ba'i sngags g.yon skor du bris nas/mu khyud dang po la las thams cad pa'i sngags bri'o//gnyis pa la ral gri'i phreng ba/gsum pa la kun tu me ri'o//de ltar dmigs 
9b2(rgyud): te mchod nas rgyal mtshan gyi rtse mo la btags na char ches pa dang ser ba 'chad par 'gyur ro//yang bsad par 'dod pas kham pa'i spar bu la ma g.yo ba'i snying po mtshal gyis g.yon skor du bris nas dkyil 'khor gru gsum gyi nang du gong du bstan pa'i ling ga'i snying gar 
9b3(rgyud): ming dang bcas pa bcug nas gan rkyal du steng du kha gzar te las thams cad pa'i sngagasabazlas shing zhag bcu drug tu las bya'o//de nas dur khrod kyis las byas pa'i mchod rten khru gang pa nag po'i bum pa'i nang du rang gis bsrung ba nyid bcug nas/mchod rten bang rim 
9b4(rgyud): gsum man chad stong pa'i nang du tsha tsha lnga bzhag pas gru gsum kha bkag cing lho phyogs su bzhag go//des zla ba gnyis na 'chi nges so//gal te gzhan gyi rig sngags gcad par 'dod na dbang chen gyi dbus su tshogs kyi bdag po bris te/zur thams cad rdo rje 
9b5(rgyud): rgya gram gyis mtshan par byas nas/grwa thams cad laM gyis mtshan pa ste/rdo rje phur pas bsgrub bya'i ling ga'i steng du mnan na/zhag bdun gyis rig sngags 'chad do//yang na hU~M bzhi mgo sprad pa'i 'khrul 'khor ro//da ni tsu tsun+d+ha ra'i pags pa la 'khrul 
9b6(rgyud): 'khor rengs pa'i sbyor ba dang bcas pa'am/sprul sdong bu byas shing tshil la mar me byas nas/du ba de bzhar nas mig la bskus na dgras mi mthong ngo //yang dpral bar thig le bya ste lag pa la bskus na gang la reg pa de dbang du 'gyur nges so//rkang pa la bskus 
9b7(rgyud): na dpag tshad brgyar 'gro nus so//sA lu'i phye ma la gzugs byas te phye ma des bskus na dgra rengs par byed do//dur khrod kyi thal ba dang bcas pas dgra bo'i sgo'am dpung la gtor na nad kyis thebs so//tsu tsun+d+ha ra'i pags pa la'akhrul 'khor bris nas/
10a1(rgyud): @#//dbugs kyis brgyangs te sngags stong bzlas na dgra de spos nas 'chi'o//de la sngags ni/tad+ya thA/na mo stu te/ma hA ga Na ba ta ye swA hA/oM ga Na ga Na a d+hi ba ta ye swA hA/auM ga NaHga Na ba ti pra dzi ti ye swA hA/na mo d+hu ti ma hA ga Na ba ta ye swa hA/oM 
10a2(rgyud): karka tra ka Ta/ma Ta ma Ta/dA ra dA ra/bi dA ra bi dA ra/ha na ha na/gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na/dA d+ha ba dA d+ha ba/ga ra ga ra/b+ha dza b+haM dza/dza b+ha dzi b+ha/mo ha mo ha/de hi di hi/d+hA pa ya d+hA pa ya/d+hA ya n+ya d+hA ya n+ya/sid d+hi me pra ya ts+tsa/sams ma/sa ma mA ra ya ma tsu sma ra ma bu tra pa tsa 
10a3(rgyud): na ya swA hA/oM ku ru ku ru swA hA/oM tu ru tu ru swA hA/oM gu ru su ru swA hA/oM mu ru mu ru swA hA/oM shAn+tiM puSh+TiM ku ru swA hA/oMg ta pr-id+hu kri bi ta tsit+ta/ma hA bi d+ha rA ga gas ts+tshin+ti/ma hA b+ha ya/par kra ma/ma hA hA sti/ma hA d+ha kri Ni ya/pra tsit+ta yA mi swA hA/oM 
10a4(rgyud): ku ru ku ru/tsu ru tsu ru/mu ru mu ru/oM gaHgaHgaHgaHgaHgaHoM na mo na maHswA hA/bzlas pa'i sngags oM brta ka swA hA/zhes bya'o//gzhan yang tshogs kyi bdag po'i sbyor ba las rlung bcing bar 'dod na gur gum dang /gi wang dang /yung ba ser pos rgyal 
10a5(rgyud): po'i ras la dbang chen gyi dkyil 'khor gyi dbyibs kyi steng du ri rab rdo rje rgya gram gyis mnan pa bris la/ya~M dang ya~M rim pa bzhin du bkod nas dpe dbus su tshogs kyi bdag po byi ba la dgod pa//bdag gser btso ma lta bu phyag bzhi pa/g.yas dang po na bgreng phreng g.yon 
10a6(rgyud): dang por rtse gsum 'og ma gnyis kyis thod pa bdud rtsis bkang ba 'dzin pa/sna nam mkha' la phyar nas blta ba bris te/de'i snying gar zla gam ljang gu'i steng du/la~M ya~M la~M bris la rnal 'byor pa dbang chen yongs su rgyas pa'i bsam gtan dang ldan pas sngags brgya rtsa brgyad 
10a7(rgyud): bzlas na rlung bcing ba'i mchog yin no//yang thog gam shing gi rtse mo la bzhag na rlung bcing bar byed do//da ni tshogs kyi bdag po yi//sna tshogs las grub 'di bshad bya//rdo rje rtse gsum gyis mtshan pa'i//dbang chen nyis brtsegs dbus su ni//zhal gcig 
10b1(rgyud): phyag bzhi rtse gsum dang //b+hi ti pA la bgrang phreng dang //a sho ka ni bzung byas te//byi ba'i steng na sems dpa' yi//skyil krung gis bzhugs khros nas ni//sngon po rin chen rgyan gyis brgyan//bsam la la dul phug dang //'o ma zho dang sbrang dkar dang //mar dang chan 
10b2(rgyud): dang sran ma sha//btsos pa'i sha dang shing tog rnams//dus dang dus su mchod byas na//gter rnams 'di yis ster bar byed//yang na bdag nyid mi g.yo ba//zhal gsum phyag drug khros pa'i sku//ral gri mda' dang rdo rje g.yas//zhags pa gzhu dang 
10b3(rgyud): mdung thung g.yon//khams gsum gang ba'i hUM las bskyed//sna tshogs mdog can khro bo ni//dum mtshon cha rnams bzung ba//rang las spro zhing bsdu byed pa'i//mdun du tshogs kyi bdag po ni//zhal gcig phyag gnyis pad+ma la//bzhengs te mda' dang gzhu 'dzin 
10b4(rgyud): pa//bskyed nas btsos pa'i shas mchod nas//bsnyen pa stong byas las rnams 'grub//yul gyi tshwa dang sa gsum la//tshogs bdag de yi gzugs byas nas//gnod pa'i ming dang ldan bcug na//lo nyes mi ngas phyugs ngas sogs//gang gi mtshan nas 
10b5(rgyud): bzung ba de//gnod pa kun las srung bar byed//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las 'khrul 'khor gyi le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//las rnams grub pa'i yan lag tu//gces par gyur pa'i sbyin sreg go//tshal 
10b6(rgyud): ni gnyis te rtsub ngos dang //yid 'ong rang gi khyim du bsgrub//sa ni las kyi dbye bas shes//skyon dang bral bas thab brtsig bya//rgyal sor tshad nyi shu bzhi//de yi ngos la sor bcu gnyis//dpangs ni de yi rgyal sa ni//sor bzhi chung bar bskor bar bya//pad+ma'i 
10b7(rgyud): dbyibs can brgyan bltas nas//ral gri 'bar ba dgod par bya//zur bzhir rdo rje phyed pa bzhi//thab khung kha la rdo rje yi//phreng ba gsal por bskor bar bya//'di ni 'phrin las kun gyi'o//yang na khru gang nang gru gsum//rim gsum sum gnyis sor tshad do//nang 
11a1(rgyud): @#//cu rdo rje rgya gram bri//gsad pa la ni bsngags pa'o//til dang 'bras dang 'o ma dang //zho dang mar dang sbrang rtsi dang //ba la sha dang be shing sogs//me tog dkar po yod pa ste//dkar gsum mngar gyis bsres pa rnams//zhi ba'i las la shin tu 
11a2(rgyud): shis//sA lu nas dang gro dang ni//rgya rtsi che dang na gu la//sardza gu gul gur gum bcas//tsam pa zho dang nye'u shing //me tog gser mdog la sogs rnams//rgyas pa'i las la shin tu shis//tsan+dan dmar dang gur gum dang //bra bo til dang rgya sran dang //khre dang 
11a3(rgyud): cung tse spel ba dang //bser mo yi bdud rtsi lnga//nag khro dang tsan+dan dmar//me tog dmar po dbang la shis//bsad la khrag dang dug yungs kar//tshwa gsum ling gar bcas pa'o//gos dkar rgyan yang dkar po ste//yo byad kun kyang dkar byas dang //
11a4(rgyud): bsam pa bzang pos shar bltas pa//'dis ni zhi ba'i las rnams 'grub//rgyan gos yo byad kun ser zhing //rgyas pa'i yid byas byang du bltas//dga' ba'i dkyil 'khor bskor byas na//'dis ni rgyas pa'i las 'grub byed//rgyan dang gos dang yo byad 
11a5(rgyud): kun//kha dog dmar zhing snum gyur nas//myos pa'i yid lta bus bltas na//dbang gi las rnams 'grub par byed//rgyan gos gnag cing yo byad gnag/rtsub pa'i yid bltas khros pa'i skad//'byin cing lho yi phyogs su bltas//drag po'i las 
11a6(rgyud): rnams myur du 'grub//de la las byed pa'i dus su mi rus la byas pa'i phur pa mi g.yo bar bsgoms la/sngon du bsnyen pa song bas dang pos kha/gnyis pas snying ga/gsum pas lte ba/bzhi pas pus mo rnams su dam par gdab po//sgra tshil lam 
11a7(rgyud): thal chen nam/bag zan la ling ga byas la ske tshe dang /re lcag dang /nya'i rdog gzhob rnams brdungs la khrag dang sbyar nas grog la rdo rje rgya gram bris pa'i dkyil du ming dang sngags spel te bris pa'i snying kar bcug la/oM tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na a mu ka s+ya sta b+ha la/
11b1(rgyud): kI la ya hU~M phaT ces gdab bo//bsgrub bya'i shes pa rlung gi steng du bzhag la dbugs dang bcas pa drang ngo //gal te gso bar 'dod na phur bu dang ming bton te 'o ma blug cing /oM tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na che ge mo shAn+tiM ku ru kurban+tu ye swA hA/zhes brjod na zhag bdun 
11b2(rgyud): nas sos par 'gyur ro//yang gsod pa'i las bya bar 'dod na/hU~M las skyes pa'i mi g.yo ba long ba zhes bya ba shin tu dmar ba'i mig gcig yod pa/ral gri dang zhags pa can kha dog ljang nag rtsub cing 'khrug pa/rin po che'i brag phug tu gnas pa la dus 
11b3(rgyud): rtag par rgyun du mchod nas gru gsum thams cad kyi dbus su bsams te sha dang khrag dang dug dang gzugs brnyan rnams zhal du bsregs na myur du 'chi'o//rengs par 'dod pas rnga mo'i rus pa la dze ta ya dze ta ya zhes stong rtsa brgyad du bsreg go//lkugs par 
11b4(rgyud): 'dod na ra'i sha la b+ha ti ra b+hi ta ra zhes khri tsho gsum bsreg go//smyo bar 'dod na khyi sha dang chang dang dug sna tshogs pa la un+ma non+ma n+da/zhes stong rtsa brgyad bsreg go//dgra bo'i mig long bar 'dod pas skyer pa'i shing dang bong bu'i rtug pa la a ta ka ra zhes stong rtsa 
11b5(rgyud): brgyad du bsreg go//sha lnga bdud rtsi lnga dang /rnga mo'i rus pa la mo ha ya zhes stong rtsa brgyad bsregs na rmongs par byed do//yung ba dang tsan+dan la dz+wa ra dpa na ya zhes brgya rtsa brgyad bsregs na yams 'chad do//spal ba dzi ra dang khyi nag po'i sha la tsi ti dz+wa lAM ma pa na ya/
11b6(rgyud): stong bsregs na mdze 'chad do//khu ba dang /khrag dang /d+ha du ra la su tri tshin+da zhes stong rtsa bsregs na mngal skams so//gtum po drag po'i nga rgyal gyis//dUr ba'am gi wa dang ma lan tshwa'am//rgya skyegs bsregs na yams nad dang //'khrugs dang 'jigs 
11b7(rgyud): dang 'byung po ldog/zho dang ku mu da dang sbrang //bya rgod klad pa khwa yi tshil//'di rnams bsregs na smyon da dang //lgang dang bu mi thon pa sel//kye gzhan yang sngags kyi rgyal po 'dis//sna tshogs las rnams 'grub 'gyur zhing //brtan pa chen po'i ye shes ni//
12a1(rgyud): @#//'ga' las myur du thob byed cing //g.yo 'dod med la bla ma smra//tshig la sems zhing don mtshol//bag med g.yeng dang ser sna dang //brkam chen snying rje med pas ni//bskal pa brgya dang stong phrag tu//bsgrubs kyang nam yang mi 'grub ste//lus 
12a2(rgyud): dang ngag dang yid kyi sgo//del ba las ni gzhan tu min//bza' bcud zhim po sna tshogs rnams//rtag par nyin re dus gsum du//grogs po kun la byin pas kyang //lan gcig bla ma mchod pa yis//brgya yi char yang mi phod do//de nas de'i tshe lha'i 
12a3(rgyud): dbang po dga' rab dbang phyug la sogs pa lha'i bu drug khris khro bo chen po mi g.yo ba la bstod pa/kye ho brtse ba'i sems mnga' 'on kyang 'khrugs 'dra zhing //dga' zhing shin tu dag kyang ra ri sna tshogs spras//lha yi khams rnams kun la ral gri phyar ba'i tshe//
12a4(rgyud): lha min tshogs kun brgyal zhing rab tu skrag par snang //srid pa'i nyon mongs kun la bsdigs pa'i sdigs mdzub kyis//bdud rnams brgyal zhing skrag nas skad phyung thag ring 'bros//yi ge phyed pa a he'i ku cos log phyogs skrag/che ba yi ni shin tu che gyur khyod kyi 
12a5(rgyud): sku//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis shin tu bsngags shing bstod//klu rnams 'dus pas cha shas lus kyis phyogs par brgyan//lha dbang bu mo rnams kyis rin chen rgyan rab gsol//spyan bgrad ljags 'dri la stag lpags 'dzin pa khyod la bstod//
12a6(rgyud): bstod pa chen po des bstod nas tshangs pa chen po la sogs pa lha tshogs thams cad bcom ldan 'das la mngon par dga'o//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po las sbyin sreg gi le'u ste bdun pa'o////'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rto pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po 'bum 
12a7(rgyud): pa las dngos grub thams cad yongs su 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i rtog pa rdzogs so////paN+Di ta a tu la dA sa badzra dang /bod kyi lo tsA ba mar pa ban+de chos kyi dbang phyug grags pas bsgyur ba'o////
12b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/kro d+ha rA dza sarba man+t+ra gu h+ya tan+t+ra nA ma/bod skad du/khro bo'i rgyal po thams cad gsang sngags gsang ba'i rgyud ces bya ba//khro bo mig yo mgon po la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//khams gsum zhe sdang can 
12b2(rgyud): gzigs nas//zhe sdang thams cad zad bya'i phyir//bcom ldan thugs rje'i rang bzhin can//zhe sdang mig yo mgon por mdzad//ngo bo nyid kyis dag pa'i bdag/sngags dang phyag rgya ting 'dzin dang //rnam dag bsam gtan 'phrin las rnams//bcom ldan 
12b3(rgyud): rdo rje 'chang chen gyis//dur khrod 'bar ba 'khrugs pa'i sar//'dzin pas btud de nges zhus la//kye kye yang dag khro chen gtso//ma lus rnam dag la gnas phyir//mgon po mi g.yo'i gsang rgyud las//sems dpa' chen po'i ting 'dzin gang //ngo mtshar 
12b4(rgyud): thar pa nges par gso//de nas yang dag ye gsungs pa//dpa' bo dur khrod gnas pas legs//rus pa'i rgyan nges phyag rgya drug/gnas pa'i tshogs kyi bdag nyid de//rgyu bzhi tshad med bzhi ru gnas//ye shes rtog pa'i rtog pa'o//rgyun ma chad pa'i dam 
12b5(rgyud): pa yis//nyi zla shes rab thabs kyi gdan//sku gsung thugs kyi ngo bor gnas//gyur pa'i mtshan ldan ye shes lnga//phyag rgya drug mdzes byin brlabs nas//dur khrod ma ni 'khyud pa'o//phung po la sogs thub par gnas//pad+ma rdo rje byin gyis brlabs//
12b6(rgyud): bsrung ba'i sbyor ba med pa dang //mtshan ma bral dang 'dus ma byas//byed pa rnam med rtog pas 'das//sku ni dug 'joms gnag pa'i tshul//rdo rje zhags pa bsdigs gnas pa//g.yon brkyang gnas pa spyan rtsa dmar//pad+ma gri gug shes rab tshul//
12b7(rgyud): g.yon 'khyud tshim byed rgyan bral ba'i//thabs dang shes rab ma chags dang //tha ma byin za mnan te gnas//'bar ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus na'o//hU~M la rgya gram rdo rje gnas//e las chos 'byung grub pa mo//mtshon cha rab dra bas so//rten pa phyag rgya lha lnga gnas//mos pa 
13a1(rgyud): @#//
13b1(rgyud): spros nas ni//gtso mo thabs kyi yan lag bkang //lag pa g.yas par gshin rje'i gshed//g.yon pa gzhan gyis mi thub pa//rta mgrin kha la bsam par bya//rdo rje la ni bdud rtsi 'khyil//dpung pa g.yas par mi g.yo ba//g.yon par 'dod pa'i rgyal 
13b2(rgyud): po'o//pus mo g.yas par bsam pa ni//dbyig pa sngon po cher 'bar ba//pus mo g.yon par stobs po che//spyi bor gtsug tor 'khor los sgyur//gnod mdzes rgyal po bsam pa ni//rkang pa gnyis la dgod par bya//yang ni yab yum sbyor ba las//byang chub 
13b3(rgyud): sems sprin 'du 'phro las//hU~M tshogs khro mor spos nas ni//gtso mo shes rab yan lag dgang //lag g.yas rdo rje ro langs ma//g.yon na gzhan gyis mi thub mo//de yi khar ni khro gnyer ma//gsang bar ral pa gcig ma'o//gzhan yang dpung pa g.yas 
13b4(rgyud): gnas par//de bzhin gshegs yum rdo rje ma//g.yon du sna tshogs rin chen gyi//phyag rgya dag ni bsgom par bya//pus mo g.yas la gnas par yang //sna tshogs pad+ma de bzhin gshegs//g.yon pa las ni sna tshogs las//spyi bor nam mkha'i rdo rje ma//rkang pa gnyis 
13b5(rgyud): la sa yi ni//sa 'dzin lha mo gzhag par bya//thabs dang shes rab sbyor ba yis//byang chub sems sprin 'phro 'du las//gdod nas grub pa'i sangs rgyas rigs//byung nas lus la dkyil 'khor bkod//de dag phan tshun sbyor ba las//rjes chags chen po'i 
13b6(rgyud): sgra yis ni//bde gshegs tshogs rnams bskul bya ste//de dag la ni yang yang gzhug/oM sarba ta thA ga ta a nu rA ga Na badzra swa b+hA ba At+ma ko&haM/chu la zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bzhin//gsal ba lha yi rang bzhin ni//kun kyang zhal gsum phyag drug ste//brjid 
13b7(rgyud): pa'i khro bo gar dang bcas//yang na zhal gcig phyag gnyis par//rnal 'byor can gyis brtan par bsgom//dngos grub rab mchog thob par 'gyur//de phyir nan tan bskyed par bya//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa las lus kyi dkyil 'khor bkod pa'i 
14a1(rgyud): @#//
14b1(rgyud): rang sngags sa bon las byung ba'i//phrag dog mi g.yo mgon po ni//don yod rdo rje'i dbu rgyan can//sngo ljang ut+pal 'dab ma'i mdog/zhal ni sngo dang dmar dang dkar//zhal gdangs la ni rnam par 'gyur//gsus khyim che zhing spyan gsum pa//'bar ba'i 'od 
14b2(rgyud): kyis rnam par brgyan//sna tshogs rdo rje 'khor lo dang //gtun shing rnams ni g.yas pa na//sdigs mdzub zhags pas phyag mtshan cing //dril bu dgra sta g.yon na bsnams//g.yon brkyang ba yis gnas pa yis//pad+ma nyi ma mnan nas ni//phyogs bcu dag tu 
14b3(rgyud): gzigs nas ni//bgegs chen 'jigs pa bsal mdzad nas//byang gi phyogs su bzhugs par gyur//de ltar phyogs bcu'i bgegs kyi tshogs//mthar byed rab tu zad mdzad nas//sems can yongs su btul byas te//zhing rnams sangs rgyas tshogs 
14b4(rgyud): kyis bkang //'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa las byung ba'i dkyil 'khor bkod pa'i le'u ste lnga pa rdzogs so//@##//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya a tsa la ma hA gu h+ya tan+t+ra/bod skad du/'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i gsang rgyud 
14b5(rgyud): chen po//'phags pa 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad chen po 'og min gyi gnas na rab tu dgyes shing bdud 'joms pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs 
14b6(rgyud): so//de'i tshe na byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal gzhon nur 'gyur ba dang /rdo rje chos dang /phyag na rdo rje dang /sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /rdo rje gnod sbyin dang /rdo rje khu tshur la sogs te rab tu dga' ba'i tshogs dpag tu med pa dang thabs 
14b7(rgyud): cig tu bzhugs te/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis badzra rak+Sha rak+Sha hU~M zhes gsungs te rdo rje khro bos phyogs mtshams gtams so//bsrung ba'i don du rab brtson zhing //bgegs kyi sbyor ba brlag par mdzad//hU~M phaT de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi sa haM zhes gsungs te rang 
15a1(rgyud): @#//gzugs ka nas khro bo'i rgyal po mi g.yo ba phyung ste/g.yas na ral gri g.yon sdigs mdzub//char sprin lta bur shin tu gnang //sbrul dang thod pas yang dag brgyan//mi bskyod pas ni byin brlabs shing //bde gshegs 'od zer dpag med 
15a2(rgyud): 'phros//dngos grub bde pa kun stsol mdzad//oM badzra sid+d+hi hU~M/de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//A li kA li rab sbyor zhing //zla ba rdo rje sbyor ldan pas//yang dag rdzogs te mngon sangs rgyas//sna tshogs rdo rje'i dbus su ni//brkyang bskum tshul du yang dag gnas//dga' 
15a3(rgyud): byed ma ni mdun du ste//rin chen phreng ma g.yas su dgod//glu mkhan mo ni g.yon du ste//brtson 'grus gar byed rgyab tu gnas//'gugs byed ma ni shar du ste//rnam par 'ching ma lho ru dgod//gnyis med ma ni nub tu ste//rnam par 'phel ma byang du dgod//'di 
15a4(rgyud): dag ni bcom ldan 'das sku yongs su rdzogs pa'i dam tshig go//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rgyud gsang ba chen po las gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dbang bskur ba'i dam tshig rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs 
15a5(rgyud): te/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas 'od zer spros te lha thams cad kyi dbu la 'od zer thim par gyur te/rigs kyi bdag pos dbu brgyan par gyur to//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//dkyil 'khor chen po dam pa ru//mchod pa'i tshogs ni rnam par 
15a6(rgyud): bstim//gzugs dang sgra dang dri dang ro//reg bya lha mo dpag med de//mchod pa'i tshogs kyis yang dag mchod//kye kye mtha' gnyis rnam par ma g.yos shing //sprul pa'i gzugs kyis 'gro don dpag med mdzad//'jigs byed chen po 'gro ba kun sgrol zhing //
15a7(rgyud): srid pa gsum na dgyes par gar mdzad pa//srid pa'i chu bo skems shing mi mthun bdud rnams 'joms//srid pa'i rlung gis ma g.yos thugs rje mi g.yo mgon//rab brjid mi g.yo mgon la phyag 'tshal bstod//bcom ldan 'das kyi bstod pa chen po'i 
15b1(rgyud): dam tshig go//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//rgyal po rtag ldan thod pa ru//lcags kyu lnga sogs gzhag par bya//rlung dang me yi dkyil 'khor bzhag/thod pa ru ni yang dag gnas//bcom ldan 'das kyi bdud rtsi chen po'i dam tshig go//de nas ya nga dag bshad bya 
15b2(rgyud): ba//tshangs pa brgya byin gshin rje dang //chu bdag gnod sbyin khyab 'jug sogs//me dang srin po rlung gi lha//'byung pos yi lha mo dang //lha ma yin sogs 'khor bcas dgug/oM a kA ro mu khaM sarba d+harmA NAM A d+ya nut+pan+na twata swA hA/oM khA hi khA hi pheM pheM swA hA/
15b3(rgyud): bdud rtsi sha dang khrag la sogs//gtor ma thams cad sbyin par bya//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rgyud gsang ba chen po las mngon par rtogs pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad byab//thugs kyi dkyil 'khor dam pa ni//khru bzhi pa 
15b4(rgyud): yi tshad du bya//sar pa las ni bkal nas kyang //dkyil 'khor nyis 'khor ring thung la//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor thig gdab po//'khor lo lta bur byas nas kyang //rdo rje phreng bas yongs su bskor//gru bzhir bgyi zhing sgo bzhir bgyi//rta babs bzhi dang 
15b5(rgyud): yang dag ldan//dbus su ral gri chen po dgod//shar du rdo rje yang dag dgod//lho ru rin chen phreng ba ste//nub tu chos kyi pi bang ste//byang du rdo rje rtse gsum mo//sgor ni lcags kyu zhags pa dang //lcags sgrog dril bu mkhas pas bzhag/bcom ldan 'das kyi dkyil 
15b6(rgyud): 'khor chen po dgod pa'i dam tshig go//de nas char pa mi 'bab na//stegs bu gru bzhi'i steng du yang //dngul gyi gzhong ba bzhag nas kyang //bag zan las ni byas pa'i gzugs//pad+ma sum brtsegs dag tu bya//steng du klu yi gzugs bzhag la//gur gum 
15b7(rgyud): gyis ni byugs byas la//mtshams su oM dang ba~M bkod de//tsan+dan dkar pos sbyin bsreg bya//kha dog mang po sbyor ldan pas//klu yi gzugs ni nges par dgug/des kyang 'bab par ma gyur na//kham pa'i gab tshe dkar po ru//pri yang ku yi khu ba yis//
16a1(rgyud): @#//ral gri brgyad pa'i 'khor lo bri//rgya mtsho'i ngos las sa blangs la//klu yi gzugs ni mngon byas te//nam mkha' lding gi khu bas byug/til mar la ni sbyin bsreg bya//de nas yang dag bshad byas//kham phor ser po'i nang du ni//bsgrub bya gzugs gzhung 
16a2(rgyud): ldong ros kyis//ri rab rtse mo brgyad pa bri//des ni yang dag gnas par bya//shin tu rno ba'i lcags dag la//khru do tshad kyi ral gri bya//maN+Dal dkar po'i steng du bzhag/zla ba drug ni rig pa bzlas//til mar gyis ni bskus byas nas//zla ba gzas zin tshe na 
16a3(rgyud): bsgrub//thogs pa'i mkha' la lding bar 'gyur//bdud rtsi lnga dang sha sna lnga//grib ma dag la bskams byas te//lcags gsum dag gis dkris byas te//shar bltas zas ni spyad par gnas//gzas zin tshe na kha rag zhug bya//mnyam par gzhag ste nyi shu bzlas//
16a4(rgyud): khar bcug gzhan gyis mngon mi 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das dngos grub chen po'i dam tshig go//de nas yang dag bshang bya ba//sa gzhi dkar la zlum pa la//tsan+dan dkar po'i me sbar te//ser skya'i mar gyis sbyin bsreg bya//dngos grub chen po thob par 
16a5(rgyud): 'gyur//yungs kar gyis ni sbyin bsreg bya//rig pa 'dzin pa'i dngos grub 'thob//til nag pos ni sbyin bsreg bya//sdig pa thams cad zhi bar 'gyur//dUr ba'i myu gus sbyin bsreg bya//tshe yi rig 'dzin thob par 'gyur//seng ldeng dag la phur ba bya//
16a6(rgyud): gsang sngags nyi shu gcig bzlas nas//'dod pa'i gzugs la dbab par bya//dgyes pas dngos grub ster bar 'gyur//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rgyud gsang ba chen po las dngos grub kyi le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das 
16a7(rgyud): yongs su dgyes nas 'jam dpal la sogs pa rnams la rnam par gzigs nas/oM AHhU~M zhes bya ba'i sgra gsungs te mi snang bar gyur to//de nas rab tu dga' ba'i tshogs kyis rang gi bdag po mthong bar ma gyur te/phyogs bzhi nas mngon du phyogs nas 'di yis 
16b1(rgyud): gsol ba btab bo//im bcom ldan ngo mtshar che//stong pa'i ngang la ci ste bzhugs//bdag ni tshangs spyod byams pa ste//mya ngan 'das pa kun nas slong //e ma'o bde ba chen po'i sku//tshig kun gyis ni ma spros pa//bdag ni rjes chags snying rje che//'gro 
16b2(rgyud): ba yang dag sgrol bar mdzad//e ma dri med dag pa'i sku//stong pa'i ngang la ma bzhugs par//rab tu dga' bas bdag la rol//'gro ba'i don la rjes chags brjod//e ma'o don kun grub mdzad pa//dag pa'i ye shes dri med sku//bdag gis 'gro kun 
16b3(rgyud): gzung ba'i phyir//gtso bo khyod ni yang dag bzhengs//de nas bcom ldan 'das rab tu dgyes te sku yongs su rdzogs par mdzad nas gsang sngags kyi rgyal po 'di dag gsungs so//oM Ar+ya a tsa la hU~M phaT/oM badzra kI li kI la ya/sarba big+h+nAn ban d+ha ya hU~M phaT/gsang 
16b4(rgyud): sngags dang phur bu'i sngags so//oM na maHsa man+ta badzra NA~M badzra tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na s+pho Ta ya/hU~M hU~M hU~M tra Ta hU~M/ma~M ba~M ha~M ma~M ta sha hU~M/ma ma hU~M phaT swA hA/shin tu rno ba'i sngags te las thams cad pa'o//de nas 'khor rnams kyis gsang sngags kyi don khong du chud par gyur to//
16b5(rgyud): 'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rgyud gsang pa chen po las gsang sngags kyi le'u ste bzhi pa'o////'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rgyu gsang ba chen po rdzogs so//@##//rgya gar skad du/badzra a mr-i ta tan+t+ra/bod skad du/rdo rje bdud rtsi'i rgyud/dpal 
16b6(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang /gsung dang thugs kyi snying po rdo rje bdud rtsi gsang ba'i pad+ma la bzhugs so//gzhal yas brtsegs pa'i khang pa na//
16b7(rgyud): mA ma kI dang thabs cig tu//bcom ldan rdo rje can rol te//de nas lha mos de zhus pa//gsang chen zhes bya cher gnas pa'i//rdo rje bdud rtsi rgyud kyi mchog/sngon du lha khyod kyis gsungs pa//bdud rtsi yi ni sgrub pa'i thabs//bde ba chen pos bka' stsal 'tshal//de nas bcom 
17a1(rgyud): @#//ldan rdo rjes bshad//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde chen po//bsam med zad med za mo ste//don yod dbang po las 'das pa//dam bzhi ba rnam dag pa//rdo rje bdud rtsi brjod pa yin//de nas bcom ldan rdo rje yis//mA ma kIr chags dang ldan nas//rdo rje bdud rtsi'i ting 'dzin 
17a2(rgyud): gyis//gsang chen mngon sum du ni mdzad//rgyud rnams kun la gsang ba yi//ye shes rdo rje bdud rtsi mchog/de ni bdag gis bshad par bya//brtan por 'khyud dang 'o byed dang //dga' ba'i rol pas mnyam sbyor dang //dbyangs dang sel snyan gar rnams kyis//rdo rje bdud 
17a3(rgyud): rtsi rgyud kyi mchog/de ltar myur du 'grub par 'gyur//bdud rtsi khu ba zhes gsungs te//'gro ba gsum ni nges bskyed do//khyod la dga' ma zhal bzang ma//de yi sgrub pa bdag gis bshad//nyon cig de yi sems kyis chog/mdor bsdus nas ni bshad par bya//sems can don du 
17a4(rgyud): phan don du//sbyor ba'i rdo rje bdud rtsi mchog/rnal 'byor gang gis shes gyur na//'khor ba'i bcings las myur bar grol//bstan du med cing ye shes che//dngos grub 'jig rten gsum sgrub bo//sku gsung thugs kyi bsgrub don du//sgrub pa pos ni bde ba spyod//
17a5(rgyud): rdo rje pad+ma mnyam sbyor bas//rdo rje bdud rtsi phyung bar bya//b+ha ga'i pad+mar 'o btab ste//rdo rje mA ma kIs 'o btab//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba che//bcom ldan rdo rjes de gsungs so//b+ha ga'i pad+mar 'o btabs pas//de tshe dga' ba'i lha mo des//rdo rje bdud rtsi las byung ba'i//
17a6(rgyud): nyams dga' gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor zhus//cho ga'i rim pa gang gis ni//mchod pa dkyil 'khor brtsam par bya//'dir ni mchod pa ji ltar te//de bzhin dkyil 'khor ji ltar lags//dga' ba chen po thob dka' ba//rab tu dgyes nas bka' stsal 'tshal//
17a7(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan rdo rje yis//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde pa che//'o btab pas ni dkar 'gyur te//bzhad nas 'di skad bka' stsal te//sku gsung thugs kyi bsgru don dang //'khor ba'i rgya mtsho bsgral ba'i phyir//dga' ba chen po sbyor ba yis//mA ma kIs ni 'dir 
17b1(rgyud): bris pa//zur gsum dkyil 'khor bris nas su//gsang ba'i pad+ma mA ma kI//nang du snying po dang ldan pa'i//pad+ma 'dab brgyad pa ni bya//de dbus gsang chen rig ma dang //gtso bo zhal gsum phyag drug pa//dang po'i zhal ni nag po ste//g.yas pa la ni dkar 
17b2(rgyud): po 'dod//g.yon du dmar por rab gsungs te//lha yi rgyan gyis brgyan byas pa//lha yin bza' me tog phreng //lha yi dri yis byug byed pa//lha yi dngos grub byed zhi ba//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje can//phyi rol du ni dkyil 'khor yang //sgo bzhi pa ni rab tu 
17b3(rgyud): brtags//shar du khro gnyer ma bzhag ste//lho na 'jigs par 'jigs byed ma//nub na rta yi gzugs can ma//byang du tshogs kyi 'dren ma mo//mkhas pas pad+ma 'dab brgyad la//rig ma brgyad ni mnyam por bri//mdzes pa mo dang zhi ba'i zhal//zla ba mo dang dung can 
17b4(rgyud): mo//ri bong 'dzin dang dkyil 'khor mo//ri bong 'dzin ri mo yid dga'//yid la bde bar byed ma dag/rig ma brgyad dang dge ma brgyad//thams cad zhal gsum phyag drug pa//sna tshogs gzugs ni yid dga' ma//phyogs rnams dag ni mtshams dag 
17b5(rgyud): la//rtag pa ru ni bri bar bya//mchod pa pi wang rnga de bzhin//de bzhin spos dang me tog sogs//dbyangs dang gar dang mchod pa ni//dga' ba rol ba sbyor ba yis//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba che//gsang ba'i 'khor lo bsgrub par bya//ma dang bran mo bu mo dang //
17b6(rgyud): sring mo gnyen mo de bzhin te//rdo rje bdud rtsi de dag las//blangs nas gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor mchod//ma las bdud rtsi blangs pas ni//rdo rje bdud rtsi dang mnyam 'gyur//sring mo nad med na so gzhon//bu mo yi ni 'dod kun sbyin//bram ze mo ni don yod grub//
17b7(rgyud): gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor la 'dod byas//lce ni dril bar byas nas su//kha yis pad+mar blang bar bya//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde pa che//de nas myur bar dngos grub bo//bung brtse ba'i tshul gyis ni//bud med thams cad 'dod pa spyod//'dus dang gru mo dang brkyang ba//yar bteg 
18a1(rgyud): @#//zer ni shin tu brkyang //byed pa'i bcing ba sbyor ba yis//sa gsum dag ni bsgrub par bya//sha chen dang ni khrag chen gyis//de bzhin bshang gci bu ba dag/de dag rdo rje bdud rtsi lnga//nyin dang nyin du bsgrub par bya//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba che//gal te 
18a2(rgyud): dngos grub bdag 'dod na//de yis don rnams thams cad ni//sgrub pa pos ni mchog tu bsgrub//dbang dang dgug dang 'thab pa dang //gsod dang bskrad pa la sogs pa//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde chen pos//bya ba thams cad kun du bya//rdo rje bdud rtsi bden chen po//
18a3(rgyud): bcom ldan rdo rjes de gsungs so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rdo rje can rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba chen po'i rdo rje bdud rtsi rgyud kyi rgyal po las gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor byed pa'i mngon tshul bstan pa'i le'u dang po'o////de nas mgu ba'i 
18a4(rgyud): lha mo de//pad+ma rgyas pa'i mA ma kI//brtan po 'khyud pa byas nas su//dbang po thams cad la'o btab//ma mchu sos bzung 'o btab ste//sid sgra sngags ni brjod par bya//rdo rje bcings pas sku yang 'dzin//rdo rje can gyis gsang byas te//pad+mar rdo rje bcu gnas su//
18a5(rgyud): chags na dga' ste 'dod par bya//sid sgra snyan po sgra rnams dang //kau k+ki le dang gling bu sogs//lha mo mA ma kI dang rtse//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde chen pos//nu ma bzung la gzir byas te//sen mo ri mos gdab par bya//de yang gos ni phud byas te//steng 'og la yang blta 
18a6(rgyud): bar bya//rgyas pa'i pad+mar 'o btab nas//dga' ba'i tshig kyang brjod par bya//rgyas pa'i pad+ma mA ma kI//lha mo chen mo legs so legs//gtan du 'dod la chags pa yis//bdag gis khyod la dga' bar bya//gang zhig 'dod pa khyod kyis zhus//khyod la bdag 
18a7(rgyud): gis mchog tu sbyin//de nas lha mo mA ma kI//tshig 'di dag ni ched du gsol//bcom ldan nyid kyis de bzhin nyid//byang chub sems kyis rnam sprul pa//phra bas shin tu phra ba yis//zad med ye shes ji ltar rol//de 'dra lha yis spyod pa ni//dbang po gnyis kyi snyoms 
18b1(rgyud): 'jug pas//de dag the tshom 'byung bas na//bdag ni ngo mtshar dam par 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan rdo rje yis//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde chen bshad//nyon cig de nyid mA ma kI//ye shes sbyor ba 'bigs byed pa//shin tu phra ba gsang chen po//ye shes shes bya ngo bo nyid//de 
18b2(rgyud): ni bdag gis bshad bya ste//sangs rgyas byang chub rab bsgrub pa//mi 'jigs zad med rtag pa ni//gdod ma med cing dbang po spangs//yod nyid dang ni med nyid kyang //dbang po dang bcas dbang po med//'byung ba kun tu ma mthong ba//sbyor ba lam gyis mthong ba'o//
18b3(rgyud): sbyor ba rnam pa gnyis gsungs te//phyi dang nang du bcas pa'o//phyi ni gzugs kyi phung po sogs//'byung ba rnam pa kun rdzob ste//sna tshogs g.yo dang mi g.yo ba//khams gsum dang ni ma lus par//de gcig pos ni khyab pa ste//thams cad rnam 
18b4(rgyud): shes kho na'o//ci phyir sems can bdag med rnams//rnam shes phung po brtan pa yin//blun sems snyigs ma'i mi rnams kyis//'ga' yang shes pa ma yin no//sems las ma byung sems kyi ngos//ye shes shes bya'i ngo bo nyid//thams cad rnam shes 
18b5(rgyud): gcig nyid do//gang tshe lus la rkyen nyid 'gyur//chos rnams dang ni chos gcig 'gyur//dngos po dag ni de tshe 'gyur//ci yi phyir na chos rnams kyi//mang po nyid du chos rnams kyang //chu rgyun bzhin du rgya mtsho la//thar pa ni gcig kho na ste//mang po nyid 
18b6(rgyud): du mi rnyed de//ji ltar nang du cher gnas pa//bla ma'i zhal kho na las thob//b+ha ga'i pad+mar nam mkha' gang //nam mkha' lnga yis brgyan pa ste//snying dang snying po rnam ldan zhing //'dab ma brgyad kyis rnam par bklubs//de dbus rdo rje can bzhugs te//byang sems 
18b7(rgyud): bdud rtsi dang ldan pa//cha shas chos min rnam grol ba//thig le de yis rnam par spangs//der gnas rtag tu zag pa ni//bdud rtsi byang chub sems kyi gzugs//khams rnams kun gyi gzhi yin pa//steng 'og tu yang rnam par khyab//des ni mthong ba thos pa yang //
19a1(rgyud): @#//ma nam pa dang ni myang ba yang //reg dang bde ba'i longs spyod ni//zas dang me tog phreng ba dang //rgyan dang gos kyis brgyan pa ste//ngag gis sna tshogs grags pa yang //blang ba dang ni gzung ba rnams//rol dang kun tu 'gro ba dang //'dug dang de bzhin nyal 
19a2(rgyud): ba dang //sad pa dang ni log pa dang //thams cad rmi lam mthong ba ste//bzhad dang de bzhin cho de dang //shin tu yid 'phrog gar dang ni//rnga rnams dag kyang brdung ba ste//sna tshogs pa dag 'dir brjod pas//smyon pa dang ni myos pa'i gzugs//gti mug 'dod chags de de 
19a3(rgyud): nyid//gzugs med mang po'i gzugs can te//pho dang mo dang ma ning rnams//du ma'i gzugs can kun gyi bdag/sna tshogs gzugs ni nor bu bzhin//khyab pa'i dngos kyis khyab nas su//de nyid dam pa cher gnas so//de bzhin nyid de mA ma kI//bzlas med 'dzin 
19a4(rgyud): pa yang med de//bgreng phreng bya ba 'ga' yang med//zas spangs pa yang mi bya'o//b+ha ga'i pad+ma mchod byas te//mkhas pas bdud rtsi bza' bar bya//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba che//de yis de nyid 'grub pa'o//gang de bdud rtsi 'bab pa'i rtsa//b+ha ga'i dbus su pad+ma ste//rtsa 
19a5(rgyud): gzhan sum cu rtsa gnyis la//de yi dbus su de gtso ste//de dag las gzhan shin tu mchog/rtsa dag dgu ni rnam par gnas//snod gul gnas rig pa dag/sgrub pa pos ni mchod par bya//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga lha mo//rtag tu rdo rje can mnyam blta//de bas de dag 
19a6(rgyud): rab byed na//chags bral zhes gsungs kun rig pas//de dag gsang chen mchog rab ni//zhal bzang ma kye sbyor bar bshad//sna tshogs rnam snang mdzad sogs pa//de bzhin gshegs pas mi shes te//gtan du dga' ba'i yid bde ba//sbyor bas dam pa rab bstan to//
19a7(rgyud): gsang ba'i pad+ma mthong nas dang //shin tu rgyas pa'i mA ma kI//rdo rje'i bdud rtsi bde ba che//denyid bdag gis bshad par bya//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rdo rje can rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba chen po'i rdo rje bdud rtsi rgyud chen po las de bzhin nyid sbyor 
19b1(rgyud): pa ye shes bstan pa zhes bya ba le'u gnyis pa'o////
20a1(rgyud): @#//shing chen gyis//yal ga brgya phrag stong can dang //rtsa dang de la gzung ba ni//skye ba'i rkyen du 'gyur ba bzhin//de ltar bdud rtsi 'bab pa'i kla//sngags dang phyag rgya'i yum yin te//sngags 'di rnams kyi skye ba ni//b+ha ga'i dbus su gnas pa'o//de nas bcom 
20a2(rgyud): ldan rdo rje can//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde chen pos//rdo rje bcug nas mA ma kI//nyon cig sngags rnams mtshan nyid ni//dang po rnam snang mdzad sbyor te//oM badzra a mr-i ta ma hA su kha//sa bon bzhi dang mnyam ldan la//swA hA yi ge yang sbyor ro//rnam par snang mdzad yang 'bul te//
20a3(rgyud): yi ge gnyis dang bzhi pa ste//bzhi pa yis ni mnyam ldan pa//mA ma kI yi snying po'i mchog/brgyad dag kho na'o dbus ma gang //brgyad la rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//'dab brgyad la ni sbyar byas te//lha mo sngags kyis sngags phyung ngo //sgo srungs dag gi sngags 
20a4(rgyud): rnams ni//nyon cig skal bzang mA ma kI//de yi tha ma sngags phyung ste//sa bon brgyad ni sngags rig pas//stong pa'i lha yis gnon byas te//rnam par snang mdzad dang ldan la//swA hA yi  ge mnyam par ldan//dngos grub thams cad rab sbyin rnams//bzhi ni sgo srungs 
20a5(rgyud): kyi yin te//bzhi ni spos dang buSh+pe sogs//ma ning yi ges grwa bzhi la//swA hA rnam par snang mdzad yang //lha mo gsang ba'i mA ma kI//rnga sogs kun gyis mchod par bya//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde chen po//panyams ldan rdo rjes de gsungs so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
20a6(rgyud): cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rdo rje can rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba chen po'i rdo rje bdud rtsi rgyud chen po las sngags phyung ba'i rim pa bstan pa zhes bya ba'i le'u gsum pa'o////de nas sbyin sreg las dag gi//cho ga'i rim pa bshad par bya//sbyor ba'i rgyud kyi cho ga yis//sku 
20a7(rgyud): gsung thugs ni sgrub pa yin//zhi ba dang ni rgyas pa'i las//de bzhin dbang dang mngon spyod dang //'thab dang bskrad kyang de bzhin du//sngags dang phyag rgya'i bsgrub pa rnams//de kun bdag gis bshad par bya//rdo rje bdud rtsa 'byung ba'o//rdo rje sems dpa' 'gying byed pa//
20b1(rgyud): bkur brten bcas pas gnyis gnas pa//rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan byas te//slob dpon dkyil 'khor 'jug par bya//khams gsum rnam par rgyal gyur nas//rkang gnyis g.yas brkyang bya ba dang //g.yon brkyang bya ba yang byas te//rkang mnyam dang ni sa ga yis//
20b2(rgyud): sin sbyong bar byed ma thag/sa la reg na 'jam skye'o//de nas bgegs rnams bskrad nas su//gzhan yang srul po la sogs pa//gong du bla ma phyag 'tshal lo//phyi nas sa yi lha mo dgug/oM e h+ye hi lha mo Ne//sa yi brtan ma 'jig rten yum//rin chen 
20b3(rgyud): thams cad rab rdzogs pa'o//lha yi rgyan gyis brgyan byed ma//ha ru nu pur sgra 'byin ma//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu mchod//yon 'di dag ni bzhes nas su//sbyin sreg las ni 'grub par mdzod//hi hi hi hA swA hA/bshang gci khrag dang khu ba yis//dkyil 'khor 
20b4(rgyud): sa la gtor bar bya//byin brlab bya ba'i phyir spos ni//sha chen gyis ni dbul bar bya//de nas rig ma 'jug byas te//gdol pa mo dang sme sha ma//gar mkhan ma dang bram ze mo//de kho na bzhin rgyal mo dang //dmangs rigs mo dang rgyal rigs mo//dri dang spos 
20b5(rgyud): kyis mchod byas te//lha yi rgyan gyis brgyan par bya//yan lag lnga la yi ge hri//b+ha ga'i dbus su yi ge bru~M//spyan ma sgrol ma gos dkar ma//yid dga' ma bdag bsgom par bya//b+ha ga'i dbus su slob dpon gyis//me tog gtor ba kho nar bya//de nas sngags 
20b6(rgyud): pas hom khung ni//tshad mar kun tu bri bya ste//zhi la zlum por bya ba ste//khru gang tsam du thig gdab po//khru phyed sa la brkos nas su//dkar rtsi tshon gyis byug par bri//kun tu 'khor lo tshul ltar yang //glo ru mnyam por bri ba'o//rgyas pa la ni khru do ste//de la khru 
20b7(rgyud): gang brko bar bya//gru bzhi pa ni mtha' mnyam du//mugs dang btsag gis bri ba'o//mngon spyod la ni zur gsum ste//sor nyi shu ni yangs pa'i tshad//nyi shu phyed ni brko byas na//dbus su rdo rje rgya gram yang //'bar ba phreng gis 'khrugs pa bri//de bzhin dbang dang 
21a1(rgyud): @#//dgug pa la//zla gam yang dag bri ba'o//hom khung yang dag ston nas su//phyogs kyi cha yang bstan par bya//byang du mngon du kha ba sgyur te//sngags pas zhi ba brtsam par bya//rgyas pa la ni shar du yang //mngon spyod la ni lto na ste//dbang ni nub tu kho 
21a2(rgyud): na bshad//dgug dang bskrad kyang de bzhin no//mngon spyod rtag par nag po ste//tshon lnga yis ni dbang sogs bya//'bras dang til dang 'o ma dang //mar dang sprang rtsi lhan cig par//bdud rtsi lnga dang ldan pa yis//zhi ba rgyas pa sbyin sreg bya//bsreg shing rnams 
21a3(rgyud): kyang thams cad la//dri zhim 'o ma can shing ngo //sna tshogs zas sam gtor ma yang //'byung po thams cad sbyin par bya//gtor ma gang yang sgrub pa pos//khrag 'thung phyag rgya chen pos sbyin//gang zhig yid kyi 'dod pa ni//rtag tu rnal 'byor pa yis 'grub//dbyar gyi 
21a4(rgyud): dus ni zhi ba ste//rgyas pa de bzhin dgun gyi dus//dpyid ka mngon spyod las rnams ni//sgrub pa pos ni thams cad bya//shar dus zhi ba rab tu gsungs//rgyas pa de bzhin srod la ste//nyin phyed la 'am nam phyed la//rtag par mngon spyod bya ba'o//hom khung 
21a5(rgyud): dbus su yi dam lha//yi ge hU~M gis bskyed nas su//zhal ni gdengs pa bsam byas te//sbyin sreg de la dbul bar bya//sbyin sreg 'bum phrag phul gyur na//mdun du mngon sum kho nar 'gyur//dang po me ni dgug bya ba//lha thams cad kyi mchod pa'i gnas//
21a6(rgyud): 'od zer phreng 'khrugs drag po'i lha//sangs rgyas byang chub 'grub pa'o//oM e h+ye hi/'dir gshegs gdon mi za bar khyod//me lha mngon sum dngos grub sbyin//drag po khams gsum gyis mchod pa//khyab 'jug tshangs pas phyag 'tshal ba//'di dag yon 
21a7(rgyud): yang bzhes nas su//zhabs g.yon brjes kyang rdo rje can//oM hU~M hU~M hU~M phaT phaT swA hA/dang po la ni yon dbul te//phyi nas lha bshos me tog dbul//oM lha yi me tog shin tu dmar//dri zhim shin tu mdzes pa ni//me tog de ring bzhes nas su//'di la byin gyis 
21b1(rgyud): brlab par gsol//hU~M me tog gi sngags so//oM mar me 'od zer 'bar ba ni//ngan 'gro 'jigs pa 'joms byed pa//lA yi mig byed bkra shis pas//mar me khyod kyis bzhes su gsol//hU~M mar me'i sngags so//oM tsan+dan ga pur dang bcas pa'i//dri zhim spos 
21b2(rgyud): me dge ba ni//mthu dang bcas par bya ba 'di//bzhes nas byin gyis brlab tu gsol//hU~M spos kyi sngags so//oM sna tshogs lha yi dri zhim pa//sna tshogs ga pur rnam brgyan pas//sna tshogs yi ge hU~M las byung //dri 'di 'bul te bzhes su gsol//hUM dri'i 
21b3(rgyud): sngags so//dang por mchod pa'i cho ga ni//the tshom med par me la bya//phyi nas rang gi yi dam lha'i//bzlas sngags rig par brjod par bya//dbyangs dang gar dang zas rnams kyi//rnam pa du mar rol byas te//rig ma dang ni sgrub pa pos//tshogs la mchod pas mnyes 
21b4(rgyud): par bya//gal te dngos grub dag 'dod na//sku gsung thugs ni 'grub pa'o//bla ma me lha rdo rje 'dzin//mchod par byas nas rdzogs par bya//oM me lha las ni grub par mdzad//sems can thams cad bde ba 'dren//sku gsung thugs dngos grub don du//lha 
21b5(rgyud): rnams las ni rjes su skyobs//me lha zhi bar gshegs nas su//sa gzhi zhi bar mdzad mdzod cig/rgyu dang mi rgyu bcas pa yis//sgrub pa po dang thams cad dang //bdag gi bsod nams 'bras 'gyur dang //sngags kyi dngos grub rab sbyin cig/a A i I u U 
21b6(rgyud): r-i r-I/l-i l-I/e ai/o au/aM aHhUM swA hA/rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba che//bcom ldan rdo rjes de gsungs so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rdo rje can rdo rje bdud rtsi bde ba chen po'i rdo rje bdud rtsi rgyud chen po las sbyin sreg gi cho ga bstan 
21b7(rgyud): pa zhes bya ba'i le'u bzhi pa'o////khyod la mig sman bshad par bya//tshil chen gyi ni snying po yi//ban da la ni dad pa blang //'ug pa'i mgo bo bsegs nas su//khrag chen dang ni bsres par bya//mtshan mo la ni btags nas su//zhib mor phye mar kho nar bya//bya rgod rje dar 
22a1(rgyud): @#//bkang nas su//mi yi rul ba'i thur mang gis//mig la bslu ba nas gzugs kun ni//rtag tu rnal 'byor pa yis byed//rdo rje bdud rtsi las byung ba'i//mig sman dngos grub sbyor ba ni//nag po'i chos brgyad bcu bzhi la//b+ha ga'i dbus su bsgrub par bya//zhi 
22a2(rgyud): ba dang ni rgyas pa dang //de bzhin dbang 'gugs las byed do//sna tshogs drag po'i gdon rnams dang //zhag bzhi pa yi rims dang dug/thams cad med par byed lha mo//dga' ma khyod la bden par bshad//rgyal po'i khab na chen por mchod//dgra bo rnams kyang zad byed 
22a3(rgyud): de//de bzhin bgegs kyang 'gag par byed//mig sman ldan par bzla bar bya//oM 'dod pa'i gzugs can mig yangs ma//bshang gci khrag la dga' byed ma//zhal gsum phyag drug pa'i lha mo//drag po'i gzugs can mA ma kI//hrIM hrIM hUM hUM a aMH/a hA hAM swA hA/
22a4(rgyud): dang po'i zhal ni nag po ste//de kho na bzhin g.yas ni dkar//g.yon ni dmar por de rig nas//g.yas phyogs ral gri 'khor lo can//g.yon du zhags pa dang lcags kyu//de bzhin rdo rje dril bu yang //b+ha ga'i dbus su bsgom byas te//de la mig sman bsgrub 
22a5(rgyud): par bya//b+ha ga gur gum gyis bkang nas//phyi nas ling ga gzhug par bya//de la brjod par bya sngags ni//oM yig sgra yig phye ba dang //badzra a mr-i ta zhes bkod//sgra sgrogs sprin dang phaT yi go go/bdud rtsi dang ni mnyam ldan pas//b+ha ga'i dbus nas dbyung bar 
22a6(rgyud): bya//de la sngags 'bum bzlas nas su//de yis thig le kho nar bya//bud med kyis khyo 'khol 'gyur te//mdun na 'don bcas rgyal po yang //skyes pa gang gis de byas na//bud med gang la skal can 'gyur//rgyal po'i khab can chen por ched//dgra bo rnams ni zad byed do//

22b3(rgyud): yi//sha chen gyis ni sbyin bsreg bya//dur khrod kyi ni bsreg shing yang //shing gcig pa dang lam mdo na//me yi hom khung bris nas su//nyin phyed nam phyed sbyin bsreg bya//khro chen gshed rje'i gshed po yi//sku la thal bas bskus pas ni//bsgoms nas

23a1(rgyud): @#//cad zhal gsum ste//de bzhin sgo srungs lcags kyu dang //de bzhin me tog spos me ma//dri yang yang dag bri bar bya//sgeg mo phreng ba de bzhin dbyangs//gar mkhan ma yang phyag drug nyid//de bzhin gling bu ma bri ste//pi wang ma dang rnga chen ma//rdza rnga ma dang 
23a2(rgyud): sil snyan kun//dkyil 'khor la ni bri ba'o//rdo rje bdud rtsi rgyud chen las//skal pa bzang po yang bzhag ste//phyag gnyis pa la zhal gcig pa//bzang po de dag kho na'o//rgyan ni thams cad kyis brgyan pa//sgrub pa po yang mdun bri'o//bgrang phreng de bzhin rdo rje dang //dril bu 
23a3(rgyud): 'dzin pa bri bar bya//gtor ma'i gtor ma mchod rdzas dang //chang skyems ro dang dga' ston mchod//nag po'i brgyad dang bcu bzhi la//dkyil 'khor la ni rdul tshon gdab//dbus su gru bzhi bri ba'o//gal te dngos grub mchog 'dod na//sngags yig gcig pa gtso bo ni//

23a5(rgyud): sngags pas rtag tu bsgrub par bya//rdo rje sems dpa'i dkyil 'khor ni//rdo rje bdud rtsi las byung ngo//bdud rtsi lnga dang mnyam ldan pa//ngan song thams cad med byed pa//rdo rje se gol mnyam sbyor gyis//dpal ldan rdo rje hUM mdzad 'grub//rnal 'byor pa dang rnal 'byor ma'i//byang

23a7(rgyud): blang bar gyur na 'jig 'gyur nas//mthu yang 'jig nas 'bras bu med//rig ma chen mos kyang phyung nas//rdo rje la yang 'o gdab bo//de ni sangs rgyas nyid du 'grub//khu ba'i gzugs can bdud rtsi ni//rdo rje bdud rtsi bde chen po//bcom ldan rdo rjes de gsungs so//de bzhin

25b4(rgyud): slong ba ni//'gags nas shi ba mo rnams ni//mtshan thams cad ni kun rdzogs pa//gdol pa mo dang sme sha can//khyim tshos ma rnams de dag na//mdzes pa bzang ba rma med pa//sum cu rtsa gnyis bsngags pa ste//lo ni nyi shu rtsa lnga bskyed//bud med ro yi skra

27b1(rgyud): rnam par spangs//de nyid gsum ldan yang dag lha//'jig rten mgon po 'gro ba'i bdag/'dod pa'i dbang phyug 'dod pa'i lha//mthong dang ma mthong 'bras rab ster//de 'dir sngags ni mtshungs med pa//bla ma'i zhal la rnam par gnas//drag chen 'jigs byed lus la ni//lnga pa'i

27b3(rgyud): yis//gtsang ngam yang na mi gtsang rung //lha bzhin mthong ba lha rnams kyis//de yi lha mo 'dod lha bzhin//'di bzhin sngags kyi 'dod lha yi//bya ba med cing sgrub pa'ang med//mchod med me la sbyin sreg med//rnal 'byor ldan dang yang dag ldan//dus tshod rnams

27b5(rgyud): song ba'i//de nyid gzhan dang gzhan du 'gro//dbye ba nyis 'gyur de las ni//ye shes nyid ni kun 'gro bya//mi 'gyur dga' ba'i yul rig pa//sems kyi rjes su rtogs pa can//bya ba de yi bde 'dod phyir//rtogs pa kun nas 'jug par 'gyur//de yi

34a1(rgyud): @#//'das pa zhes bya ba de ni bde ba can du 'gro ba'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis smras pa/ci lag na rdo rje sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams chos sku spangs nas bde ba can du 'gro ba'am/rigs kyi bu 'on te sprul pa'i sku spangs nas longs spyod 
34a2(rgyud): rdzogs pa'i skus bde ba can du 'gro bar 'gyur//lag na rdo rje chos kyi sku spangs nas 'gro bar 'gyur zhes bya ba de ji lta bu yin/lag na rdo rje sa smras pa/ci ltar sgyu ma mkhan gyi skyes bu gang zhig gis dgos pa'i don bsgrub pa'i phyir sgyu ma sprul nas dgos pa sgrub par byed do//

34b1(rgyud): ste//sgrib pa lnga po spangs pa'o//zab cing rgya ni che ba'i gzugs//de nyid ngo bor de nyid bzhugs//gnyis ka yod pa ma yin phyir//gdod nas yod pa ma yin no//de ni gdong nas ma skyes pas//skye ba ma yin 'gag ma yin//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis smras pa//
34b2(rgyud): lag na rdo rje sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das khams gsum la rjes su ston pa mdzad pa/skye ba med pa 'gag pa med pa ci lta bu yin/smras pa/bye byad chub sems dpa' rnams de ji snyam du sems/gang sangs rgyas ji snyed pa de gang zhig yod pa yin nam/'on te med pa zhig yin 
34b3(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyis smras pa/lag na rdo rje sangs rgyas rnams ni yod pa yang ma yin med pa yang ma yin no//lag na rdo rjes smras pa/ma skyes pa ni skye ba yin//skyes pa phyir yang skye mi 'gyur//don dam nyid du dpyad na ni//de phyir gdod nas ma skyes 
34b4(rgyud): pa//de bzhin 'chi ba 'chi mi 'gyur//'chi ba med pa 'chi ba min//mi gnas pa la gnas pa med//gnas pa de la gnas mi dgos//gal te ma skyes nyams 'gyur zhing //ma skyes pa nyid skye 'gyur na//ri bong gi ni rwa nyid kyang //skye ba dang ni 'gag par 'gyur//

34b7(rgyud): dkyil 'khor sdom pa ji lta bu//dngos grub de ni ji ltar 'grub//lag na rdo rjes smras pa/rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba ni//dngos po rnams ni 'byung ba ltar//sngags dang phyag rgya sogs brten nas//dngos grub rnams ni 'byung bar 'gyur//

35b4(rgyud): sdi ni mar khur bcas//lus kyi srin bu shi ba'i ro//zos nas dbang du 'gyur ba yin//dbang du bya ba'i sman no//dug 'gugs pas ni 'gro bzhi//shes rab 'phel bas sangs rgyas nyid//chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba ni//skad cig gis ni dbang byed 'gyur//bdag gzhan

35b7(rgyud): rab 'phel 'gyur lus kyi skyon rnams zad pas dbang du byed//de phyir 'gro 'di mi dge la zhen dngos grub thob mi 'gyur//de phyir rgyal ba'i sras kyis phan phyir sdig rnams spang bar bya phyir bgyi//phyag rgya bcing ba'i khyad par gyis ni nges par lus kyi skyon rnams zad bar 'gyur//
36a1(rgyud): @#//sngags kyis lce yi tshogs kyi skyon rnams gang yin pa yi sems kyi skyon//bsam gtan dga' ba'i sems kyis dga' bar 'gyur bar dad pa dri med kyis//lus kyi yid rnams g.yo ba med pa de tshe sangs rgyas byin gyis rlobs//sdig med mig gis sdig 
36a2(rgyud): lta snying rje ldan pa 'jig rten gsum gyi rgyal por 'gyur//sbyin pas bsod nams stobs lhag gyur par sa stengs gtso bo sbyin bdag brtson ldan 'gyur//le lo spangs pas mkhas pas dmyal ba'i sdug bsngal cung zad kyang mi myong //de phyir sangs rgyas sras kyi snying rje'i nor

36b1(rgyud): ming dbang du bya ba'i cho ga bzhin du grol 'am/yang na me tog dang bcas pa'i ras la bris la rig pa'i ril bu byas nas dpung pa la gdags par bya'o//bdag po bud med bran du 'gyur//rgyal po'ang zhabs 'bring nyid du 'gyur//shin tu dag cing gtsang ma yis//sngags 'di
