86b4(rgyud): can khams rnams kyang //gzhug rnams su ni bstan bcos tshad mar byas//gdod nas dag pa'i go 'phang mkha' mnyam mtshungs//sangs rgyas lnga yi cod paN dang yang ldan//gang zhig chos de nyid kyi rang bzhin can//sa gsum po yi chos ni 'byung 
86b5(rgyud): 'gyur ba//rdo rje sems dpa' de la phyag 'tshal lo//rdo rje sems dpa' la ni phyag 'tshal ba'i//bsod nams kyang ni mtha' yas 'byung 'gyur zhing //rdo rje sems dpa' de yang phyag 'tshal bas//chos kyi bdag nyid chen po 'thob par 'gyur//sems can 
86b6(rgyud): thams cad yongs su bsgom par yang //bya ba 'di ni thar pa'i rang bzhin no//dngos kun rdo rje sems dpar dag byas pa'i//thar pa chos kyi rang bzhin bya ba ste//chos 'di 'jig pa med pa'i rang bzhin can//'jig rten pas ni rang bzhin gzhan du byas//
86b7(rgyud): 'di ni sku dang gsung dang thugs dag ni//chos 'di yi ni dang po 'byung ba ste//rnal 'byor gnas ni lha mo skul byed ma//der ni dang por chos rnams 'byung 'gyur ba'o//der ni sngags rnams rdo rje gcig dngos bsgom//chos kyi rang bzhin rdo rje dngos dmigs sam//
87a1(rgyud): @#//ral kha'i thor tshugs kyis kyang rab brgyan cing //sku mdog kyang ni sngon po'i rang bzhin can//rdo rje sems dpa'i ting 'dzin dge ba ste//gcig pa'i rang bzhin nyid ni bsgom par bya//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi ni dbus gnas par//
87a2(rgyud): yi ge bskyed pa nyid du bsam par bya//yi ge'i gzugs ni yongs su gyur pa las//rdo rje rang bzhin nyid du bsam par bya//mchu yi mtha' ni sngags pas bsgom bya ba//rang gi ngo bo nyid kyis rang bzhin te//gnyis su med pa'i sku yi rang bzhin can//rdo rje
87a3(rgyud): sems dpa' dmigs par bya ba'o//'di ni sems nyid yongs su grub pa ste//rdo rje 'dzin pa yis ni gsungs pa yin//gang zhig bsgom pa 'di yis sgom byed pa//sems kyi rang bzhin nyid ni 'grub par 'gyur//'di ni lha mo bzhi rnams ro gcig 
87a4(rgyud): pa'o//ngo bo blos ni mkha' dang mnyam pas bsgom//de lta'i chos byas sems kyi rang bzhin te//lha mo thar pa'i rang bzhin nyid du bsgom//rdo rje sems dpa' des mthong ba//chos rnams kun gyi rang bzhin can//rnal 'byor gnas dang rol pa ste//
87a5(rgyud): thams cad mnyam pa'i btsun mor bcas//bkod pa dag dang ldan par gnas//mkhas bcas dngos las byung de la//rdo rje sems dpa' gang mthong bar//gzung ba sangs rgyas spro bsdu'o//bzhi yi ngo bo lha mo bzhi//dbus su nges par gnas 
87a6(rgyud): pa ste//sgyu ma lta bu'i rang bzhin gyis//sa gsum la ni khyab pa'o//lha yi der ni kha bzhi pa'i//lha tshogs rdo rje nyid yin no//de la sangs rgyas rnams kyi ni//rnam 'phrul thams cad 'di yi'o//ci lta ci ltar sems can rnams//skye bar 
87a7(rgyud): 'gyur ba lan nyid de//sangs rgyas rnams kyi rnam 'phrul ni//sna tshogs rdo rje yin par 'gyur//de las gang byung lha kun kyang //rdo rje sems dpa'i rnam 'phrul yin//sems can rnams ni chos kyi ngo bor blta//sa gsum thams cad gcig pa'i ngo bo nyid//
87b1(rgyud): rol pa mdzad pa sangs rgyas kyis gzigs shing //de yis rnal 'byor dbang phyug ma kun mthong //lha yi dam tshig gi le'u ste dang po'o////rtag tu mchog ldan khyod kyi ni//dam tshig lha mo thams cad kyi//mchog tu gyur pa'i lho mo ni//du mchis bdag 
87b2(rgyud): cag zhu bar 'tshal//de la glu bzhi gsungs pa ste//steng dang dbyer med byas pa'i chos mnyam rang snang ma//zhi la thar pa ma dang mchog tu mnyam byed ma//gang phyir sgyu ma'i mdzad pas sangs rgyas rnams dang mnyam//mkha' mnyam thams cad rol ba zhi ba 
87b3(rgyud): med pa'o//ru lu ru lu b+h+yo ru d+ha//mkha' dang mnyam pa'i chos rnams kun du rol ba ste//sgyu ma'i rang bzhin dang ni sgra brnyan chos mnyam snang //lhan cig gnas min cha ste rang nyid pi wang mtshungs//byas pa'i snang ba dang ni mnyam par gyur pa'o//gang zhig chos rnams snang 
87b4(rgyud): ba nam mkha' dang mnyam pa//der ni rdo rje mdzad pa gzugs can lta bu ste//rdo rje'i theg pa der ni gzugs sogs 'dod yon gyi//bde ba mi zad sna tshogs dang mnyam stsol mdzad pa'o//rnal 'byor gnas ni du ma'i rdo rje'i tshul 'di ni//du ma'i gzugs 'dzin 
87b5(rgyud): der ni rdo rje dang mnyam zhing //nyid dang 'dra ba'i sems dpa' rnams ni spro mdzad pa'i//rdo rje dbyings dang lhan cig mchog stsol mdzad//rang bdag mkha' mnyam bzhugs la bskul ba ni//gang zhig rtse gcig sems kyis 'di nyan cing //brjod bcas 
87b6(rgyud): sems kyi glu 'di 'dzin pa'o//glu 'di nam mkha' dang ni mnyam pa ste//der ni gzugs bcas rdo rje len pa'i phyir//rdo rje'i bdag nyid thugs ni sad byed cing //rang bzhin gcig pa'i phyir ni rgyud kyang len par 'gyur//'di ni rdo rje'i tshig ste rdo rje gzhan 
87b7(rgyud): rang bzhin//sngags pas thugs chud dkyil 'khor kun bsgoms shing //chos kyi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas pad mchog der bsgom pa//dkyil 'khor dbus gnas chos ni hU~M gi dbye ba can//gzugs rnams thams cad sgyu ma'i ngo bor nges par brtag/

90a1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/sarba ta thA ga ta kA ya bA kyi t+ta ra ta s+yo gu h+ya sa mA dza nA ma ma hA ka lwa bA dza/bod skad du/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs kyi gsang chen gsang ba 'dus pa zhes bya ba brtag pa'i rgyal po chen po/dpal rdo rje sems

90a7(rgyud): dpa' sems dpa' chen po/byang chub sems dpa' ba'i sems dpa' chen po chos kyi dbyings kyi ngo bo nyid rdo rje dang /de dag la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' ched po brjod kyis mi lang ba'i yang brjod kyis mi lang ba'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul 
90b1(rgyud): phra rab snyed rnams dang /de bzhin gshegs pa nam mkha'i khams dang mnyam pa la yang 'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje mi bskyod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs ba rdo rje rnam par snang mdzad dang /de bzhin gshegs la rdo rje rin chen tog dang /de bzhin gshegasapa rdo rje 'od

90b3(rgyud): kyis nam mkha'i khams thams can shin tu yongs su gang bar snang bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rjernam par snang mdzad chen po de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi 'dod chags chen po'i tshul zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas 
90b4(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi bkod pa de dag nyid kyi sku la zhugs par mdzad do//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad kyis bcom ldan 'das de bzhan gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs kyi bdag po yongs su mnyes par bya ba'i phyir nyid kyi sku bud 
90b5(rgyud): med kyi gzugs su mngon par sprul nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad chen pa'i sku las mngon par byung bar gyur to//de la la la ni sangs rgyas spyan gyi rnam par ro//la la ni mA ma kI'i rnam par ro//la la ni gos dkar mo'i rnam par ro//la la nI dam tshig 
90b6(rgyud): sgrol ma'i rnam par yang dag par gnas par gyur to//la la ni gzugs kyi ngo bo'i rnam pas/la la ni sgra'i ngo bo'i rnam pas/la la nI dri'i ngo bo'i rnam pas/la lan ni ro'i ngo bo'i rnam pas/la lani reg bya'i ngo bo'i rnam pas yang dag par gnas par 'gyur to//de 
90b7(rgyud): nas de bzhin gshegs pa mi bskyod pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis btsun mo'i b+ha ga la gru bzhi du ba med pa dam tshig chen po'i dkyil 'khor byin gyis brlabs pa ni/gsal ba de yi ngo bo nyid//sna tshogs gzugs kyis kun du rgyas//sangs rgyas sprin ni kun 
91a1(rgyud): @#//tu bkram//me stag 'phro ba mang po 'khrug/gsal ba la sogs dkyil 'khor ldan//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi gnas//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje'i bdag po de/de bzhin gshegs

92a1(rgyud): @#//
92b1(rgyud): thugs kyi rig pa'i skyes bu de nyid rin chen tog gi phyag rgya chen por sbyor ba nam mkha' mchog gi gnas kyis ser po nag po dkar po'i rnam pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje'i g.yas phyogs su bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin

92b7(rgyud): nas yang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi rdo rje 'dzin pa rjes su chags pa'i dam chog ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad kyi btsun mo'i mchog 'di nyid kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje  las phyung ngo //dwe 'a ra ti/de

100a3(rgyud): bzlas//lus dang ngag dang sems rnams ni//bder gnas thob na byang chub 'thob//gzhan du dus min 'chi ba dang //dmyal bar nges par bsreg par 'gyur//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' ni//sngags kyi spyod pa mchog spyod pas//chos dang mi 'gyur 
100a4(rgyud): mchog brnyes rnams//'dod pa thams cad bsten pas so//chags can ye shes 'dod pa yis//'dod yon lnga rnams rtag tu brten//byang chub sems dpa' mnyes par bya//byang chub nyi ma chags par bya//gzugs la rnam gsum shes byas nas//
100a5(rgyud): mchod pa'i bdag nyid can mchod bya//de nyid bcom ldan sna tshogs pa//sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad gzhi//sgra la rnam gsum shes byas na//lha rnams la ni dbul bar bya//de nyid bcom ldan rnam tshogs pa//sangs rgyas rin chen 'byung gnas 
100a6(rgyud): gtso//dri la rnam gsum shes byas na sa//sangs rgyas sogs la dbul bar bya//de nyid rtsom ldan sna tshogs pa//'dod chags chos kyi 'byung gnas gtso//rol rnam gsum shes byas nas //lha rnams la ni dbul par bya//de nyid sangs rgyas sna tshogs pa//
100a7(rgyud): sangs rgyas don yod rdo rje 'dzin//reg bya rnam gsum shes byas nas////rang gi rigs la dbul bar bya////de nyid bcom ldan rdo rje can//mi bskyod pa yi tshul brnyes rnams//gzugs sgra dri la sogs pa la//rtag tu sems ni sbyar bar bya//'di 
100b1(rgyud): de sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//gsang ba'i snying po btus pa'o//gzugs sgra dri sogs sngags pa yis//lha rnams nyid du rtag tu bsgom//yang na de la rigs kyi ni//dbye ba rnam par bsgoms pas bsgom//sangs rgyas rjes su dran par sbyor//chos 
100b2(rgyud): ni rjes su dran sgom pa//lus dang ngag dang sems ni bsgom//rdo rje rjes su dran sgom pa//rigs ni rjes dran sbyor ba las//khro bo rjes su dran sgom pa//dam tshig rjes dran sbyor ba las/bsgoms na byang chub thob par 'gyur//bud med gzugs ldan mdangs 
100b3(rgyud): bzang ba//lo grangs bcu drug lon rnyed nas//byin gyis rlob pa'i gnas gsum gyis//dben par mchod pa rab tu brtsam//de bzhin gshegs pa'i btsun mo che//spyan la sogs pa bsgom par bya//dbang po gnyis ni mnyam sbyar bas//sangs rgyas 
100b4(rgyud): dngos grub thob par 'gyur//yi ge hU~M dang oM dag dang //phaT kyang rnam par brtag par bya//'od zer sna lngas khyab pa yi//rdo rje pad mo bsgom par bya//de ni rab bcas zla 'od bzhin//yid du 'ong ba bsgom par bya//sangs rgyas rjes dran sbyor la sogs//
100b5(rgyud): byang chub 'dod pas bsgom par bya//de la sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa ji ltar bsgom zhe na/b+ha gar ling ga rab bzhag nas//sangs rgyas gzugs ni bsgom par bya//ba spu'i bu ga rtse bar nas//mkhas pas sangs rgyas sprin rnams spro//de la chos rjes su 
100b6(rgyud): dran pa ji ltar bsgom zhe na b+ha gar ling ga rab bzhaganas//rdo rje chos ni rab bsgom bya//ba spu'i bu ga rtse par nas//mkhas pas chos kyi sprin rnams spro//de la rdo rje rjes su dran pa ji ltar bgom zhe na/b+ha gar ling ka rab bzhag nas//rdo rje sems dpa' 
100b7(rgyud): rnam par bsgom//ba spu'i bu ga rtse b.ranas//rdo rje sprin rnams spro bar bya//de la rigs rjes su dran pa ji ltar sgom zhe na/b+ha gar ling kha rab bzhag nas//sangs rgas gzugs ni rab tu bsgom//ba spu'i bu ga'i rtse bar nas//mkhas 
101a1(rgyud): @#//pas rigs kyi sprin rnams spro//de la khro bo rjes su dran pa ji ltar bsgom zhe na/b+ha gar ling ga rab bzhag nas//khro bo'i dbang po rab tu bsgom//ba spu'i bu ga rtse bar nas//mkhas pas khro bo'i sprin rnams spro//de la dam tshig rjes su dran pa ji 
101a2(rgyud): ltar bsgom zhe na/rang gi rdo rje pad mar ldan//dbang po gnyis ni mnyam sbyar te//rang gi khu ba'i thigs pa yis//sangs rgyas rdo rje sems dpa' mchod//de la dkyil 'khor rjes su dran pa ji ltar sgom zhe na/dbang po gnyis ni mnyam gzhag 
101a3(rgyud): ste//mkhas pas rang gi khu ba dag/rnal 'byor pa ni chen pos dbyung //dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor tshul du bya//de la sku rjes su dran pa ji ltar bgom zhe na/sangs rgyas kun gyi sku gang zhig/phung po lnga yis rab tu rgyas//sangs rgyas sku yi ro bo yis//
101a4(rgyud): bdag kyang de dang 'drar gyur cig/de la gsung rjes su dran pa ji ltar bsgam zhe na/rdo rje chos kyi gsung gang zhig/nges pa'i tshig ni phun sum tshogs//bdag gi tshig kyang de 'dra bar//chos 'dzin pa dang 'drar gyur cig/del thugs rjes su dran pa ji ltar bsgom zhe 
101a5(rgyud): na/kun du bzang po'i thugs gang zhig/gsang ba'i bdag po blo ldan pa//bdag gi sems kyang de 'dra bar//rdo rje 'dzin dang 'drar gyur cig/de bas sems can rjes su dran pa ji ltar bskom zhe na/sems can kun gyi sems gang yin//sku dang gsung dang thugs 
101a6(rgyud): kyi mtshan//bdag gi sems kyang de'adra zhing //mkha' dang mtshungs par dpa' bo rnams//de la sngags kyi gzugs dang sku dang gsung dang thugs thams cad rjes su dran pa ji ltar sgom zhe na/sngags kyi rdo rje'i sku gang yin//gsung gi sku ni rnam bsgom pa//
101a7(rgyud): bdag kyang de dang 'dra ba'i sku//rtag tu sngags 'dzin pa dang mnyam//de la dam tshig rjes su dran pa ji ltar bsgam zhe na/dam tshig khu chu cho ga bzhin//'bras bu 'dod pas btung bya zhing //de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshogs gsang bya//dngos 
101b1(rgyud): grub rab mchog thob par 'gyur//de la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i dam tshig rjes su dran pa ji ltar bsgom zhe na/thams cad rang bzhin 'od gsal ba//skye ba med cing zag pa med//byang chub med cing mngon rtogs med//khams kyang med cing 'byung ba med//
101b2(rgyud): de la ma skyes pa rjes su dran pa ji ltar sgom zhe na/rang bzhin 'od gsal de nas ni//nam mkha' mtshan med mi 'gyur des//gnyis med gnyis su med min zhi//shin tu dri med nam mkha' bzhin//de la zhe sdang gi rigs kyi mchod pa rjes su dran pa ji lta bsgom 
101b3(rgyud): zhe na/bud med sems ni rab brtan pa//lo grangs bcu gnyis lon rnyed nas//rig kyi sbyor ba'i rab dbye bas//rang gi khu bas mchod par bya//'dis ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku//rdo rje 'dzin pa'i thugs dag dang //chos 'dzin mchog 
101b4(rgyud): gi gsung dag ni//tshe 'di nyid la thob par 'gyur//sku dang gsung thugs yang dag grub//dman skyes gang gzhan bshad pa rnams//rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pas//bzlas pas de la 'grub par 'gyur//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang 
101b5(rgyud): gsung dang thugs kyi gsang chen gsang ba 'dus pa las sngags kyi spyod pa mchog gi le'u ste bdun pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rin chen tog gis bcom ldan 'das de bzhi gshegs pa thams cad kyis bdag po dam pa'i dbang phyug 
101b6(rgyud): rdo rje 'chang chen po la bstod pa'i rgyal po 'dis gsol ba btab bo//rdo rje rgyal po theg pa che//nam mkha'i spyod pas rnam sbyong ba//kun du bzang po'i spyod pa mchog/rgyal mchog mchod pa bshad du gsol//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug che//rdo rje 
101b7(rgyud): theg pa rab ston pa//nam mkha'i dbyings dang mtshungs pa'i mchog/rgyal bzhi mchod pa bshad du gsol////thar pa'i lam ni rab ston pa//theg pa gsum gyi lam 'jug mdzad//sangs rgyas skal mchog dag pa'i bdag/mi mchog mchod 
102a1(rgyud): pa bshad du gsol//byang chub sems ni rgyas pa'i spyan//chos kyi 'khor lo rab skor ba//sku dang gsung thugs shin tu dag/rdo rje theg pa khyod phyag 'tshal//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//thams cad nam mkha' mi 'gyur che//thams 
102a2(rgyud): cad dbang bskur kun gyi don//kun dbang rdo rje rin chen 'dzin//de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod pa'i mchog/rdo rje gsum ni mi phyed gnas//sku dang gsung dang thugs skal mchog/rgyal ba 'byung bar 'gyur ba bshad//na chung yangs pa'i mig can 
102a3(rgyud): ni//gzugs dang lang tshos brgyan rnyed nas//lo grangs nyi shu lnga lon gzung //byol song las kyang rab tu brtag/sa phyogs gcong zhing rnam par dben pa'am//rgyal ba nyid skyes zhi ba zhi ba'i gnas//bshang gci tshul sogs pas byug pa yang //rgyal 
102a4(rgyud): ba mchod phyir rtag par rab tu bya//nu ma'i dbus gang spyi gtsug mtha' dbus su//cho ga shes pas rkang pa'i bar du yang //lte ba ro smad gsang ba rgyal ba'i sras//rigs lnga rnams ni dgod par rab tu bya//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//
102a5(rgyud): ye shes rgya mtsho bsgom par bya//bdag nyid zla ba'i dbus gnas bsgom//mkhas pas zhi ba rdo rje 'dzin//snying gar gang zhig 'dod pa ni//bsdu ba dag kyang rab tu bya//mchod rten rin chen bzhi'i rang bzhin//'od zer phreng bas rnam brgyan pa//ye 
102a6(rgyud): shes rgya mtsho dus gsum gyi//gnas su gyur par rnam par bsam//rang gi ba spu'i bu ga nas//mkhas pas mchod pa'i sprin rnams spro//mkhas pas ut+pal dag dang //pad ma rnam pa lnga shes bya//dzA ti rnam gsum shes byas nas//lha rnams la ni dbul bar 
102a7(rgyud): bya//me tog ka ra ni ka ra dang //ma l+li ka dang yu thi ka//me tog ka ra bI ra yang //bsams nas mchod pa rab tu brtag/dpag tshad brgya yi khyon tsam du//'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor bsgom bya zhing //rnam par mkhas pas rtag par ni//rigs rnams 
102b1(rgyud): rab tu dgod par bya//de bzhin pad ma rdo rje dang //ral gri ut+pal mkhas pas bsgom//dpag tshad bye ba'i khyon tsam la//gru bzhi shin tu mdzes pa yi//mchod rten rin chen bzhi'i rang bzhin//gsal zhing rang bzhin dri ma med//rigs rnams gnas ni mchod 
102b2(rgyud): pa'i phyir//shes rab can gyis bsgom par bya//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga rnams kyis//de la mchod pa rgya mtsho brtsam//rin chen na bza' la sogs pas//byang chub 'dod pas rtag tu mchod//mchod pa rnam lnga mchog rnams kyis//rtag tu lha rnams mnyes par 
102b3(rgyud): bya//rin chen 'byung gnas na chung mchog/rin chen sna tshogs la sogs brgyan//bsgrub cing rab tu bsgrub pa'i phyir//sangs rgyas kun la dbul bar bya//rin chen bdun gyis yongs bkang ba'i//zhing 'di rnam par mkhas pas ni//ye shes 'dod pas dngos 
102b4(rgyud): grub 'dod//nyin re shes kyis can gyis dbul//bdag po'i phyag rgyar mnyam gzhag la//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor dbus dag tu//'dod chags blo can sangs rgyas la//reg bya'i sbyor ba dbul bar bya//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//dkyil 
102b5(rgyud): 'khor bkod pa bsgom par bya//rang lus de bzhin gshegs rang bzhin//bshang ba gci bas bla ma mchod//bzhin bzang yid du 'ong ba yi//na chung skal ba mchog ldan pa//byin gyis brlabs kyi gnas bsams te//de nyid mchod pa rab tu brtag/
102b6(rgyud): mig langs khu ba blangs nas ni//brtan pa'i blo dang ldan pas bza'//'di de sngags rnams thams cad kyi//sku gsung thugs kyi mchod pa ste//ye shes rdo rje can gyi gsang //sngags rnams dngos grub byed par gsungs//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang 
102b7(rgyud): gsung dang thugs kyi gsang chen gsang ba 'dus pa las bshang gci'i dam tshig gi le'u ste brgyad pa'o////
103a1(rgyud): @#//phyug dam pa yis//sku gsung thugs kyi longs spyod pa//rdo rje mi phyed gsum dkyil 'khor//sangs rgyas theg pa can la gsang //nyams dga' mchog ni rab tu bshad//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//sangs rgyas dkyil 
103a2(rgyud): 'khor bsgom par bya//mi bskyod rdo rje rab bsgoms nas//lag tu rdo rje bsgom par bya//'od 'phro mang po 'bar ba dang //'od zer sna lngas rab tu gang //dus gsum 'byung ba'i sangs rgyas rnams//bsgoms nas rdo rjes phye mar brlag/sku gsum thugs 
103a3(rgyud): kyi longs spyod ni//rdo rjes phyer brlags med gyur pa//bsam gtan mchog de bsgoms na ni//thugs kyi dngos grub thob par 'gyur//rdo rje gsang ba 'di yis ni//sems can thams cad bsad na ni//mi bskyod sangs rgyas zhing du ni//rgyal 
103a4(rgyud): ba'i sras su skye bar 'gyur//'di ni rigs thams cad kyi rgya mtsho las zhe sdang gi rigs kyi dam tshig gi de kho nar shes par bya'o//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//mi shes thar pa rab sgrub pa//ngo bo nyid dag gos pa med//byang chub spyod pa rab ston pas//
103a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas byang chub bsgrub pa yi//yang dag dam tshig bka' stsal to//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//rnam par snang mdzad rab bsgoms te//sangs rgyas thams cad rnam par bsgom//rin chen 
103a6(rgyud): kun gyi sbyor ba yis//rdo rje'i gzugs ni rab tu brtag/nor rnams thams cad phrogs nas su//rdo rje gsum gyis rnam par bsgom//yid bzhin nor bu 'dra ba yis//nor gyi rgya mtshos rab gang 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun gyi sras rnams dang //thub pa'i 
103a7(rgyud): skyes bu rnams su 'gyur//'di ni rigs kyi rgya mtsho thams cad las gti mug gi rigs kyi yang dag pa'i dam tshig tu shes par bya'o//de nas rkyal po rdo rje 'dzin//'dod chags thar pa rab sgrub pa//gsang bdag pa dmigs med pas//dkyil 'khor 
103b1(rgyud): rab tu bka' stsal to//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//pad ma'i dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//'od dpag med ni rab bsgoms te//sangs rgyas rnams kyis thams cad ni//dgang zhing de ru thams cad ni//btsun mo'i rnam pa'i 
103b2(rgyud): sbyor bar bsgom//dam tshig bzhi yi sbyor ba yis//'di ni rdo rje'i tshul mchog go//dbang po gnyis ni rab sbyor bas//de dag thams cad nye bar spyad//'di ni sngags rnams thams cad kyi//mi phyed sku gsum sgrub pa'o//'di ni 'dod chags 
103b3(rgyud): kyi rigs kyi yang dag pa'i dam tshig ste/sngags pas bsgom par bya ba nyid do//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//rdo rje sngags kyi don sgrub pa//ye shes las byung bdag med pas//gsung ni 'di skad bka' stsal to//nam mkha' dbyings kyi dbus 
103b4(rgyud): gnas par//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor bsgom par byas//rdo rje don yod rab bsgoms te//sangs rgas thams cad bsgom par bya//brdzun gyi ngag ni rdo rje'i gnas//thams cad gzugs su rnam bsgom bya//rgyal ba'i gnas ni thams cad dang //rgyal ba 
103b5(rgyud): thams cad bslu bar bya//'di de sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//nam mkha'i gsung ste dri med pa//ye shes blo can kun gyi gsang //sngags kyi dngos grub byed par bshad//'di ni dam tshig 'gugs pa'i rigs kyi dam tshig gi de kho na nyid de/don ji lta ba 
103b6(rgyud): bzhin du spro bar bya'o//de nas rgyal po rdo rje'i 'dzin//rdo rje gsum mi phyed rgyal gnas//dngos grub rdo rje rab ston pas//gsung ni 'di skadabaka' stsal to//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//dam tshig dkyil 'khor bsgom par byed//rin chen 
103b7(rgyud): rtog tu rab bsgoms te//gzugs rnams kun gyis 'di dag dgang //rtsub pa'i ngag la sogs pa yis//bsten na ye shes thob par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje bkod pas de skad ces bka' stsal to//de nas 
104a1(rgyud): @#//yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dam tshig rdo rje tog la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams ngo mtshar zhing rmad du byung bar gyur nas rdo rje'i tshig 'di smras so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi 
104a2(rgyud): bdag po khams gsum las 'das shing 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad las 'das pas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi nang du 'di lta bu rmad du byung ba'i tshig gi lam rdo rje'i tshig 'di ci'i slad du 
104a3(rgyud): gsung de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa thams cad kyis brjod kyis mi lang ba'i yang brjod kyis mi lang ba'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi ri rab kyi rdul phra mo snod kyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams? cad kyi dam tshig rdo rje dog la sogs pa'i byang chub? sems dpa' de dag la 'di 
104a4(rgyud): skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu dman pa'i 'du shes dang /smad pa'i 'du shes 'di lta bu ma skyed cig/de ci'i phyir zhe na/rigs kyi bu gang 'di sngags kyi spyod pa ni mchog gi spyod pa'o//gang 'di sngags kyi spyod pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod pa'o//rigs 
104a5(rgyud): kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na nam mkha' ni thams cad la rjes su song ba ste/chos thams cad ni nam mkha'i khongs su gtogs pa'o//de dag kyang 'dod pa'i khams na mi gnas/gzugs kyi khams na mi gnas/gzungs med pa'i khams na mi gnas 
104a6(rgyud): 'byung ba chen po bzhi la mi gnas so//rigs kyi bu chos thams cad ni de ltar rjes su rig par bya'o//'di lta bu'i don gyi dbang mkhyen nas/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams sems can rnams kyi bsam pa mkhyen nas chos 'chad par mdzad do//rigs kyi bu de lta 
104a7(rgyud): bu nyid kyi nam mkha'i tshig nges par brjod pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam tshig de dag rjes su rig par bya'o/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na gtsub shing dang /gtsub stan dang mi'i lag pa bskyod pa'i rkyen gyis du ba rab tu 'byung zhing me 'byung bar 'gyur mod kyi/
104b1(rgyud): me de yang gtsub shing la yang mi gnas/gtsub stan la yang mi gnas/mi'i lag pa bskyod pa la yang mi gnas so//rigs kyi bu de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje dam tshig rjes su rig par bya ste/'gro ba dang 'ong ba la sogs pas zhes pa'o//
104b2(rgyud): de nas byang chub sems dpa' de dag ngo mtshar du gyur/rmad du byung bar gyur pas dam zhing mig gdangs te tshig 'di skad ces smras so//rmad byung chen po'i chos rnams ni//nam mkha' dang ni mtshungs pa dang //rnam par mi rtog dag pa la//kun rdzob yang ni 
104b3(rgyud): bshad pa mtshar//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi gsang chen gsang ba 'dus pa las/don dam pa'i dag pa de kho na nyid kyi don gyi dam tshig gi le'u ste dgu pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 
104b4(rgyud): thams cad yang 'dus te/bcom ldan 'das dam tshig chen po'i rdo rje de kho na nyid mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa sku gsung thugs kyi gsang ba de bzhin gshegs pa la phyag 'tshal te 'di skad ces gsol to//de nyid sngags snying kun bsdus pa//sku dang gsung thugs 
104b5(rgyud): gsang bar bsgrags//dngos grub chen po'i tshul gyi mchog/bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad du gsol//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//nyon mongs kun gyi rgya mtsho gtso//'bar ba gtum po spyan yangs pas//gsung ni 'di skad bka' stsal to//lus 
104b6(rgyud): dang ngag dang sems rnams bsgom//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje rnams//rnam par mi rtog mi dmigs pa//mnyam nyid gar yang mi gnas pa//de nas bcom ldan 'das rang bzhin gyis dag pa'i de bzhin gshegs pas pha rol tu phyin pa sngags kyi tshul rdo rje zhes 
104b7(rgyud): bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mi bskyod pa la sogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad rig pa du ma bye ba khrag khrig 
105a1(rgyud): @#//brgya stong dang thabs cig tu sems can thams cad kyi don bya ba'i gar gyi rol pa ston par mdzad de/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' klas par 'dod pa'i yon tan lnga rnams kyis rol cing dgyes par yongs su spyod pa yod kyang de dag sngags 
105a2(rgyud): spyod pa la mngon par brtson pa rnams la spyan ras kyis mi 'tsho ste/de rgyu gang gi phyir zhe na/kye ma 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad sngags spyod pa'i tshul gyis chos thos pas so//de'i phyir skyes bu dam pa chen po de rnams kyi spyan ras kyis btsa' ba'i phyir de bzhin

110a6(rgyud): pa'i mchog ni bya//khams gsum gyi dam tshig dgug pa'o//rnam pa kun gyi mchog ldan pa'i//sangs rgyas sku ni rnam bsgoms la//lag tu sku dang gsung thugs ni//kyo ba la sogs rab tu bsgom//'di lta bu yi sbyor ba yis//de ni gnas las byed par 'gyur//rnam pa kun 
110a7(rgyud): gyi mchog ldan pa'i//rdo rje sku ni rnam bsgoms shing //lce yi rdo rje'i sbyor bas bsgom//rdo rje'i gsung dang 'dra bar 'gyur//rnam pa kun gyi mchog dang ldan pa'i//sangs rgyas skar ni rnam bsgoms la//lags tu bsgrub bya bsams na ni//rdo rje dang ni 'dra bar 'gyur//gsang 
110b1(rgyud): gsum dam tshig mchod pa'i mchog/mchod pas mchod nas rab bsgrub 'di//de ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//gsang ba'i snying po bsdus pa yin//bcom ldan 'das gsang ba chen po'i dam tshig gis de skad ces bka' stsal to//sha chen dam tshig mchog gis ni//rdo rje gsum mchog 
110b2(rgyud): bsgrub par bya//bshang gcing mchog mchog gis ni//rig pa 'dzin pa'i gtso bor 'gyur//glang po'i sha yi dam tshig gis//mngon shes lnga nyid thob par bya//rta sha dag gi dam tshig gis//mi snang ba yi bdag por 'gyur//khyi sha dag gi dam tshig gis //dngos grub 
110b3(rgyud): thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//ba glang sha yi dam tshig gis//rdo rje dgug pa'i mchog tu 'gyur//sha rnams thams cad ma rnyed na//thams cad bsams pas rnam par brtag/rdo rje'i sbyor ba 'di byas na//sangs rgyas thams cad byin gyis rlob//rnam pa kun mchog ldan pa 
110b4(rgyud): yi//sku gsung thugs ni rdo rje can//snying gar ye shes dam tshig dang //dbu rgyan rdo rje mchog 'dzin pa//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyes 'gyur la//'di ni dam tshig tshul gyi mchog/dam tshig mchog gi bya ba ni//dngos grub thams cad byed pa'i mchog/dam tshig thams cad 
110b5(rgyud): kyi ye shes rdo rje'i zas zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//lce ni dam tshig rdo rje mchog/hUM yig rdo rje can bsgoms te//bdud rtsi lnga yi sbyor ba yis//zos na rdo rje gsum thob 'gyur//'di ni rdo rje'i tshul mchog ste//AHdang o kyi dam tshig kyang //'di lta bu yi sbyor ba yis//
110b6(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra bar 'gyur//rdo rje dam tshig bdud rtsi'i phreng ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzinato//rdo rje dam tshig gsum 'grub mchog/sku gsum rdo rje can du 'gyur//phyogs bcu'i sems can thams cad kyi//yid bzhin nor bu'i rgya mtshor 'gyur//'jig rten khams ni thams 
110b7(rgyud): cad du//rdo rje'i bdag nyid snang bar byed//'khor lo'i dam tshig grub pa mchog/sangs rgyas sku dang mnyam par 'gyur//gang+gA'i bye snyed thams cad du//kun nas grub pa rnam par spyod//thams cad dam tshig mchog gis ni//rig pa 'dzin pa'i gtso bor 'gyur//thams cad dam 
111a1(rgyud): tshig gzugs mchog ni//rdo rje sku ni rab bsgoms pas//mi snang gyur pa thams cad la//stong gsum gcig pus snang byed pas//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'phrog pa dang //lha mchog bu mo dag la spyod//gang+gA'i bye snyed sangs rgyas rnams//rdo rje gsum gyi gnas bzhugs 
111a2(rgyud): pa//rang lag skyu ru ra bzhag bzhin//rdo rje'i mig gis mthong bar 'gyur//gang+gA'i bye snyed zhing rnams na//sgra grag pa ni ji snyed pa//rna ba'i drung na 'khod pa bzhin//mngon shes khyu mchog gis thos 'gyur//gang+gA'i bye snyed/zhing dag gi//sems can kun gyi sems rnams 
111a3(rgyud): ni//sku gsung thugs kyi mtshan nyid can//rol mo ltar byung sems rig 'gyur//bskal pa gang+gA'i bye snyed du//'khor bar gnas par 'byung ba yi//sngon gyi gnas kyi gtan tshigs rnams//zhag gsum lon pa bzhin du dran//gang+gA'i bye ma snyed kyi sku//sangs 
111a4(rgyud): rgyas sprin la sogs pa rgyan pas//bskal pa gang+gA'i byed snyed du//rdzu 'phrul mchog gi rdo rjes 'gengs//bcom ldan 'das dam tshig mngon par mkhyen pas de skad ces bka' stsal to//de la mngon par shes pa lnga ni rdo rje'i spyan dang /rdo rje'i snyan dang rdo rje'i thugs dang /rdo 
111a5(rgyud): rje'i gnas dang rdo rje'i rdzu 'phrul lo//sangs rgyas mngon shes don grub cing //sangs rgyas sku dang 'dra bar 'gyur//gang+gA'i bye snyed grangs med pa'i//'khor gyis yongs su bskor nas su//rdo rje'i sku dang gsung dang thugs//'jig rten khams kun rnam par bgrod//bsnyen pa'i 
111a6(rgyud): dam tshig sbyor ba dang //nye bar sgrub pa 'byung ba dang //sgrub pa'i don du dam tshig dang //sgrub pa chen po dag dang bzhi//shes byas rdo rje'i bye brag gis//de nas las rnams bsgrub par bya//bsnyen pa ting 'dzin rab sbyor ba//byang chub mchog ni bsgom par bya//nye bar sgrub pa grub 
111a7(rgyud): mchog la//rdo rje'i skye mched rnam dpyad pa//sgrub pa bskul ba rab brjod pa//sngags dang bdag po rnam bsgom pa//sgrub pa chen po byed pa'i tshe//rang sngags rdo rje can gyi gzugs//dbu rgyan la ni bdag po bsam//ye shes rdo rje 'grub par 'gyur//bsnyen pa ye shes 
111b1(rgyud): bdud rtsis bya//thams cad du ni rtag tu nyid//'di gang sngags rnams thams cad kyi//sngags bcu de nyid don sgrub pa'o//dgon pa chen po'i sa phyogs dang //rab tu skye bos dben pa dang //ri bo nags 'dab chu dogs su//rtag tu dngos grub thob par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das 
111b2(rgyud): rdo rje sgrub pa chen pos de skad ces bka' stsal to//yang na rdo rje bzhi dag gis//brtul zhugs brtan pas bsnyen par bya//rdo rje sku gsum mnyam de yis//bsgoms na dngos grub thob par 'gyur /thun bzhi rnams dang rab sbyor bas//gnas lngar blo dang ldan pa yis//oM gyi yi ge 
111b3(rgyud): bsgoms nas ni//ye shes rdo rjes sdom pa bstan//zhag bdun dang ni zla phyed dang //zla gcig zla ba phyed dang gnyis//rdo rje dam tshig bsgrubs na ni//dngos grub myur du thob par 'gyur//zhag grangs bye brag rab dbye bas//nga yis rgyas par rab tu bstan//phyogs kyi nang 
111b4(rgyud): las dngos grub gsungs//gsang ba mchog las byung rnams kyis//de la 'di ni nye bar bsgrub pa'i sdom pa'i yul lo//sangs rgyas sku 'dzin dpal dang ldan//rdo rje gsum dang mi phyed dngos//bdag la byin gyis brlab gnas deng //sku yi rdo rjes mdzad du gsol//phyogs bcur 
111b5(rgyud): bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//rdo rje gsum dang mi phyaid dngos//bdag la byin kyis brlab gnas deng //sku'i rdo rjes mdzad du gsol/de la 'di ni sgrub pa'i sdom pa'i yul mo//chos nyid gsung lam dpal dang ldan//rdo rje'i gsum dang mi phyaid dngo//bdag la byin 
111b6(rgyud): gyis brlab gnas deng /gsung gi rdo rjes mdzad du gsol/phyogs bcur bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnam//rdo rje gsum dang mi phyid dngos//bdag la byin gy-is brlab gnas de ga/gsung gi rdo rjes mdzad du  gsol//de la 'di ni sgrub pa chen po'i sdom pa'i yul lo//rdo rje 
111b7(rgyud): thugs 'dzin dpal dang ldan/rdo rje gsum dang mi phyed  dngos//bdag la byin gyi brlab gnas deng //thugs kyi rdo rjes mdzad du gsol/phyaugs bcur bzhugs pa'i sad rgyas rnams//rdo rje gsung dang mi phyaid dngos/bdag la byin gyis brlab gnas deng /thugs kyi rdo rjes mdzad du
112a1(rgyud): @#//gsol//sangs rgyas rdo rje sems dpa' 'am//yang na chos kyi sems dpa' rang //gal te rmongs bdag nyid 'da' na//tshal par 'gas 'dir gdon mi za//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi gsang chen gsang ba 'dus 
112a2(rgyud): pa las de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rdo rje'i sbyor ba dam tshig sgrub pa mchog nges par bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o////de nas bcom ldan bde gshegs kun//ye shes rdo rje mchog 'dzin dang //sems can kun don yang dag 'byung //byang chub 
112a3(rgyud): sems dpa' blo ldan gyis//thub pa don kun rdo rje can//ston pa che la phyag 'tshal nas//dam tshig de nyid mkhyen pa la//mchod de rdo rje'i dbyangs 'di gsungs//e ma'o sangs rgyas tshul re bzang //e ma'o byang chub tshul gyi mchog/e ma'o chos kyi tshul re zhi//
112a4(rgyud): e ma'o sngags kyi tshul re bstan//ma skyes pa yi chos rnams ni//ngo bo nyid kyis khyad par can//rnam par mi rtog yang dag la//ye shes 'byung bar rab tu gsungs//yid 'ong sngags rnams kun bsdus pa//rdo rje bzlas pa ye shes che//sku gsum mi phyed dkyil 
112a5(rgyud): 'khor ni//sangs rgyas ye shes thob 'gyur ba//rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pas//bzlas pa rdo rje'i sbyor ba yis//sangs rgyas kun gyi byin gyis rlob//rigs ni sngags rnams thams cad kyi//sku dang gsung dang thugs mtshan nyid//sngags kyi bzlas pa de ring gsungs//
112a6(rgyud): ye shes rgya mtsho gsan pa dang //sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje can//sangs rgyas dus gsum byung ba rnams//rdo rje sngags ni rab bsgoms pas//ye shes mnyam med thob 'gyur ba//bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad du gsol//de nas ston pa rdo rje 'dzin//nam mkha' rdo rje ye 
112a7(rgyud): shes byung //byed skyed dam pa mchog gi gtsos//rdo rje bzlas pa 'di gsungs so//sngags kun don gyi bzlas pa ni//rdo rje mi phyed gsum mtshan nyid//dbye ba gsum gyi rdo rje'i mthar//bkod pa rdo rje gsum brjod bya//sku gsung thugs kyi gnas rnams su//spro ba rnam pa 
112b1(rgyud): gsum dag bya//rdo rje bzlas pa 'di yis ni//rdo rje gsum gyi thugs 'drar 'gyur//sangs rgyas rnams kyi sku gsung thugs//bsgoms nas mchod pa'i mchog brtag pa//ye shes rdo rjes byas na ni//rdo rje gsum gyi thug 'drar 'gyur//sangs rgyasarnams kyi sku gsung thugs/
112b2(rgyud): bsams nas mchod pa'i mchogabartag pa//ye shes rdo rje rab tu bya//'di ni byang chub mchog 'thob pa'o//yang na dbye ba rnam gsum gyis//so so so sor spro bar bya//sku dang gsung thugs bdag med pa//ye shes sems kyis yang dag spro//brjod nas rdo rje spro bya 
112b3(rgyud): zhing //rdzogs nas rab tu bsdu bar bya//'di ni ye shes spyan phye ba'i//sangs rgyas rnams ni kun gyi yin//sku yi rdo rje rdzogs byang chub//dngos dang dngos med rnam dpyad pa//sangs rgyas sku zhes bya bar gsungs//de ni sku yi bzlas par bshad//gsung gi dam tshig rdzogs byang 
112b4(rgyud): chub//sgra dang sgra min rnam dpyad pa//gsung gi rdo rje zhes gsungs te//de ni gsung gi bzlas par bshad//thugs kyi dam tshig rdzogs byang chub//gnas pa'i rdo rje rnam dpyad pa//thugs kyi rdo rje zhes gsungs te/de ni thugs kyi bzlas par bshad//don gyi rjes 
112b5(rgyud): rtogs bzlas pa ni//ngo bo nyid byung bzang po yis//dus gsum sangs rgyas rnams dpyad pa//de ni rin chen bzlas par bshad//sangs rgyas zhing las thams cad du//sku yi sprin rnams spro ba ni//bzhud cing byon pa rdo rje'i don//de ni don yod bzlas par bshad//khro bo'i 
112b6(rgyud): dam tshig shes pa yis//rang gi rdo rjes bsgrags dkyil 'khor//sngags kyi yi ge'i tshig thos pa//de ni khro bo'i bzlas par bshad//'dod pa'i don gyis myos gyur pa//sems can kun la phan dga' med//sems can gti mug gnas la 'jog/de ni gti mug 
112b7(rgyud): bzlas par bshad//'dod chags rdo rje las byung tshig/lus dang ngag sems la gnas pa//sems can 'dod chags mtshor gnas bzhag/de ni 'dod chags bzlas par bshad//zhe sdang rdo rje las byung sems//lus dang ngag sems lag nas pa//sems can zhe 
113a1(rgyud): @#//sdang gnas la bzhag/de ni zhe sdang bzlas par bshad//dam tshig rdo rje gsum de nyid//dam tshig rdo rje can dbu ma//de nyid dam tshig rdo rje yi//ma ning bzlas pa zhes bshad do//'dod chags de nyid don bsgom pa//rdo rje bdag po thams cad ni//sems 
113a2(rgyud): can kun la phan 'dod pa'i//'dod chags las skyes byang chub mdzad//rig pa chen po spyan la sogs//rtag tu 'dod pa'i don brtson pa//'dod pa'i longs spyod dag gis ni//ji ltar 'dod pa bsten na 'grub//gti mug dam tshig las byung ba//rig pa'i rgyal po 
113a3(rgyud): rdo rje can//ma ning dag gi gnas grub pa//dngos grub dam pa rab tu ster//khro bo zhe sdang gnas las skyes//rtag tu gsod la brtson pa rnams//gsod pa'i don gyis sgrub pa po//mchog gi chos can gyis 'grub 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das skyes bu chen po'i dam 
113a4(rgyud): tshig gis de skad ces bka' stsal to//ye shes 'khor lo can gyis ni//snying ga'i dbus su 'khor lo bsgom//gsal ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas pa//dbus su 'khor lo'i don bsgom mo//ye shes rdo rje can gyis ni//snying ga'i gzugs su rdo rje bsgom//rdo 
113a5(rgyud): rje dkyil 'khor dbus gnas ba//rdo rje'i sngags don bsgom par bya//rin chen ye shes can gyis ni//snying ga'i dus su rin chen bsgom//rin chen dkyil 'khor dbus gnas la//rin chen sngags don bsgom par bya//pad+ma ye shes can gyis ni//snyig ga'i dbus

113b6(rgyud): tshig las byung ba'i//sangs rgyas 'khor lo rnam bsgoms la//sangs rgyas 'khor lo stobs po che//lag pa g.yas pas 'di bya'o//phyogs bcu nas byung sems can rnams//sangs rgyas sku yi bye brag gis//bsdus te gong bu'i tshul gyis su//de dag rang gi lus la

114a1(rgyud): @#//par byed//rdo rje sems dpa' rang nyid kyang //sangs rgyas sku gsum mchog stsol bas//rdo rje gsum gyi dkyil 'khor gnas//dngos grub ster 'gyur rmongs bdag nyid//'di la the tshom mi bya'o//'di ni nyin zhag bdun bya ste//sangs rgyas kyis kyang

114b5(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das ngo bo nyid dag pas de skad ces bka' stsal to//rdo rje chu gter rkang pas gnon pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dz-in to//ra bus su dbang chen dkyil 'khor ni//bsams te khro bo'i rigs bzhag la//las kyi rdo rje gnas gnon pa//de yi spyi bor bsgom par

125a1(rgyud): @#//byung //dam tshig mchog gis ldang bar 'gyur//nyams pa'i gnas su mi 'gyur na'o//sku gsung thugs kyi sbyor ba yis//cho ga yang dag byas tsam gyis//mkha' dbyings rdo rje mtha' klas par//phur bus 'debs 'dir gdon mi za//bcom

125a6(rgyud): bdag nyid che//bu mo lo grangs bcu gnyis pa//dben pa'i gnas su khyad par du//sgrub pa pos ni rtag tu bsgrub//bshad gci'i dam tshig la sogs pas//gru bzhi pa ni cho ga yis//dkyil 'khor bya'o de ru ni//rdo rje dkyil 'khor bsgoms pa yis//

125b1(rgyud): sngags kyi 'khor lo can gyi gsang //mchod pa'i gnas ni nyams dga' bsgom//sku gsung thugs ni rdo rje can//rnam par snang mdzad sku chen po//bsam gtan sngags kyi sbyor ba yis//sangs rgyas kyi ni 'od 'drar 'gyur//btso blag mkhan ni bdag nyid che//
125b2(rgyud): bu mo ut+pal mthing ga'i mdog/'dra ba dag ni rtag tu bsgrub//rdo rje sems dpa'i sbyor ba yis//de nyid sbyor ba'i cho ga ni//byas nas las ni brtsam par bya//'di ni sngags rnams thams cad kyi//dam tshig shin tu 'da' dka' ba'o//de ni de ma thag nyid du//rdo rje 
125b3(rgyud): sems dpa'i 'od 'drar 'gyur//chos rnams kun 'dzin rgyal po mtshungs//'dod dang thar pa rab sgrub pa//rdo rje chos ni rnam bsgoms pas//gar mkhan bu mo rab mdzes shing //bzhin bzang mig ni dkyus ring ba//sgrub pa pos ni rtag tu bsgrub//de ni rdo rje

125b5(rgyud): pa bsgrub//rdo rje nyi ma nub nas ni//sgrub pa kun du brtsam par bya//skyabs shar ba'i dus su ni//bsgom pa mchog gis 'grub par 'gyur//rgyan rnams thams cad yongs su rdzogs//dri dang me tog rnams kyis brgyan//bsgoms na rdo rje sems dpa'

130a2(rgyud): 'dug bsam pa//'od zer lnga ni rab rdzogs pa'o//sdud pa na ni dam tshig gnag/bskul ba na ni dkar po 'dra//bsam gtan gnas 'di gsang ba ste//gsang chen ye shes dri ma med//de la 'di ni phyi nang gi nad rnams gso ba'i rdo rje snying po'i sngags kyi yi ge'i tshig rnams so//

179a7(rgyud): de ni rtag tu bsgom//gang phyir yid ni dgar gyur pas//de bas de nang yid 'ong ba'o//gnas med gnas par legs sbyor bas//bzhi mtshungs gcig tu sbyor ba rig/sangs rgyas rjes dran sbyor la sogs//byang chub 'dod pas bsgom par bya//
179b1(rgyud): nyams dga' sangs rgyas kun gyis bstan//b+ha ga zhi ba gcig pu'i ming //rang gi de nyid gnyis med byung //rdo rje de bdag nyid du sbyor//sangs rgyas rjes dran sbyor ba ste//b+ha ga ling+ga yin par bsgrags//gzhug pa gnyis med sbyor ba ste//sangs rgyas 
179b2(rgyud): gzugs su bsgom pa'o//de las blo ldan gyis spro ba//ba spu'i bu ga'i rtse mor gnas//mnyam pa med pas khyad par du//sangs rgyas sprin rnams der spro'o//chos rjes dran pa'i sbyor ba yang //rdo rje chos kyi rab sbyor bas//rdo rje pad+ma yang dag sbyar//
179b3(rgyud): b+ha ga'i ling+gar brtag pa'o//de nas ba spu'i bu ga las//'bad pas chos kyi sprin rnams spro//de nyid bsam kun rdzogs byed pa'i//'di ni chos kyi bdag po yin//rdo rje rjes su dran pa bsgom//dngos med gcig pu la sbyor ba//stong la rdo rje rab sbyor 
179b4(rgyud): 'gyur//b+ha ga ling+ga zhes bshad do//de nas ba spu'i bu gnas//rdo rje sprin rnams kun du spro//rdo rje byang chub sems bsgom nyid//myur du dngos grub 'thob par 'gyur//rigs rjes dran pa'i sbyor ba yis//mkhas pas stong nyid rdo rje las//de bzhin rnal 
179b5(rgyud): 'byor pas spros na//rigs kyi dngos grub thob par 'gyur//khro bo rjes dran sbyor ba la'ang //stong dang rdo rje gnyis ldan pa'i//khro bo'i ba spu'i bu ga nas//khro bo'i sprin rnams spro bar bya//de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'dzin//khyab bdag rnam snang mdzad 
179b6(rgyud): chen la//rjes dran sbyor ba'i dgongs pa ni//thub pa che la slar zhus pa//thub khyod kyis ni gang gsungs pa//b+ha ga'i ling+ga'i sbyor ba ni//'jig rten 'das pa'i sbyor 'di las//sangs rgyas la sogs spro gyur pa//tshul 'di ma 'ongs skye bo la//phyi dus kun 
179b7(rgyud): rmongs bskyed pa ste//bde gshegs rnams kyi bdag pos ni//ci phyir gsungs zhes de rnams smra//'gro bas nam mkha' mtshan ma can//rten 'byung khyod nyid mi shes shing //de rnams shin tu rmongs dbye bas//ji bzhin par ni rtog par byed//mgon khyod 
180a1(rgyud): @#//bde ba chen po ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi ston gcig pu//sku med srid gsum bdag nyid can//dgongs pa gnyis ka'i mtshan dang ldan//de bas khyod ni chos kun gyi//yon tan gsum bdag de nyid gsum//dgongs pa bya ba dri med gsum//sangs 
180a2(rgyud): rgyas kyi ni bdud rtsi 'byung //khyod 'dir b+ha ga khyab 'jug ste//yid+ga drag po'i ming can nyid//khyod ni sa bon tshangs la brtag/khyod ni rten 'byung 'gro ba 'byung //dus gsum las 'das rnal 'byor che//dus dang cis su gyur pa nyid//zhes dang shes bya rnam 
180a3(rgyud): par grol//de bas khyod ni 'gro ba'i bla//de nas rdo rje 'dzin pa yis//de la sogs pa'i tshig gsan nas//dam tshig rjes su dran bsgom pa//bcom ldan 'das ni ston par mdzad//rang gi rdo rje pad+mar ldan//dbang po gnyis ni rab sbyor las//rang gi khu 
180a4(rgyud): ba'i thigs skyes pa'i//sangs rgyas rdo rje sems dpa' bsgom//dkyil 'khor rjes su dran pa dag nyid//dbang po gnyis nyid sbyor ba las//rang gi tshangs pa rtag drangs te//rnal 'byor pas ni dkyil 'khor bya//sku rjes dran pa'i sbyor ba yang //sangs 
180a5(rgyud): rgyas rnams kyi phung po lnga//sangs rgyas de nyid sogs brtag pa//bdag kyang de dang 'dra bar rig/gsung rjes dran pa'i sbyor ba lnga//rdo rje chos gsung phun sum tshogs//de yi gsung ci 'dra bar 'gyur//bdag kyang de dang 'drar chos 'dzin//thugs rjes 
180a6(rgyud): dran pa'i sbyor ba gang //rdo rje 'dzin pa'i thugs dang ni//kun du bzang po'i thugs gang yin//bdag kyang de dang 'dra? bar 'gyur//sems can rjes dran sbyor ba gang //sems can rjes dran rdo rje nyid//spyod pa mkha' mtshungs ji bzhin du//bdag kyang de dang pad 
180a7(rgyud): bar 'gyur//sngags sku rdo rje gsum zhes pa//de sbyor dran pa bsgom pa ni//rdo rje sngags kyi sku gang yin//gsung dang sku ni rnam sgom pa//de nyid gsum mnyam gcig pa nyid//rtag tu gsum 'das gyur pa yi//sngags 'dzin mchog ni ci 'dra ba//bdag kyang de dang 'drab par 'gyur//
180b1(rgyud): dam tshig rjes dran rnams kyis ni//de bas tshangs pa shin tu dbyung //dngos med gcig tu sbyor ba yis//mkhas pas cho ga bzhin du gsung //de bzhin gshegs mchog che ba ni//dngos med ces bya bas da ba ste//blo dang ldan pas 'di bsad na//dngos grub sngags mchog 'thob 
180b2(rgyud): par 'gyur//shes rab dam tshig sbyor gyur pa//rjes su dran pa zhes bshad do//pha rol phyin pa zhes grags pa//yod med gnyis dang bral ba'o//thams cad rang bzhin dri ma med//ma skyes pa dang zad pa med//byang chub med cing mngon rtogs med//khams med 'byung ba'ang 
180b3(rgyud): med pa'o//ma skyes rjes su sbyor ba gang //der ni ci yang brjod min zhing //ma skyes ming ni gcig pu pa//dran pa de ni yin par bshad//ji ltar nam mkha' 'od gsal ba//mtshan ma med cing 'gyur pa med//rang bzhin mkha' dang 'drar gyur cing //gnyis med gnyis su yod min 
180b4(rgyud): zhing //yan lag bcu gnyis sbyor gcig gang //zhe sdang rdzogs longs spyod pa'i ming //sangs rgyas kun gyis bsten gyur cing //de ni rigs rjes dran zhes bya//rten 'byung las skyes rdzogs sangs te//bud med lo grangs bcu gnyis pa//brtan pa'i sems ni thob pa las//rang gi khyad 
180b5(rgyud): pas mchod par bya//dngos med bdag nyid dag pa gang //de ni dag par bshad pa yin//de nyid kyis ni mchod par bsngags//shes rab pha rol phyin pas 'gro//'di yis lus la mchod pa dang //khyad par du ni de bzhin gshegs//rdo rje 'dzin bshad de bzhin thugs//chos 'dzin 
180b6(rgyud): pa yi sku gsung thugs//sku gsung thugs ni yang dag grub//tshe 'di nyid la thob par 'gyur//gang bshad rigs ni dman bshad pa//de la'ang tshul de grub par 'gyur//de nyid mchod par bshad pa nyid//de bzhin so sor rab tu brtag/mi phyed rdo rje'i sbyor ba yang //gsung gi dbang bskur ba nyid 
180b7(rgyud): gsal//dgongs pa lung bstan la gnas nas//gang zhig sems ni ma rmongs pa//mkha' la gnas pa'i nyi ma bzhin//de ni ci dgar spyod par byed//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//rdo rje gsum gyi gsang ba ste//sngags spyod pa yi mchog gyur pa//le'u bdun pa rab tu bstan////
181a1(rgyud): @#//de nas dpal ldan de bzhin gshegs//dkon mchog dpal ni 'od chen po//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//bla ma rdo rje gsum gyi bdag/mgon po rdo rje 'dzin chen po//dbang phyug dbang phyug dam pa dang //sangs rgyas kun gyi ston pa la//bstod pa 'di yis 
181a2(rgyud): gsol pa btab//nam mkha'i spyod pa rdo rje mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' thub pa che//theg pa chen po nam snang ba//kun tu bzang po bzla la zhugs bzang //dngos kun dngos po gcig pa'i mchog/rigs mchog thams cad yang dag 'byung //rgyal ba'i dam pa'i mchod pa'i mchog/
181a3(rgyud): mchod pa kun mchog bshad du gsol//'dod chags chen po zhe sdang che//gti mug chen po blo gros che//theg pa gsum po rab ston cing //rdo rje theg pa ston pa po//nam mkha'i gcig pu dngos po'i mchog/ye shes rgya mtsho rnams che//rdo rje gsum po chags med pa//rgyal gnas mchod pa 
181a4(rgyud): bshad du gsol//rdo rje gsum po lam gyi mchog/thar lam gcig pu ston pa po//sangs rgyas skal mchog shin tu dag/mi mchog mchod pa bshad du gsol//dngos po kun gzigs shin tu dag/byang chub sems ni rgya mtsho che//chos kyi 'khor lo gcig bskor ba//rten 'brel 
181a5(rgyud): ye shes las byung ba//rdo rje gsum gcig rnam dag don//sna tshogs sangs rgyas bya ba mdzad//bdag med chos ni shin tu dag/phyag 'tshal rdo rje theg pa la'o//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa dag po//thams cad nam mkha' mi 'gyur che//thams cad dbang bskur kun dbang po//
181a6(rgyud): kun don rin chen mchog 'dzin pa//na chung mig yangs btsal nas ni//gzugs dang na tshod rnams kyis brgyan//lo grangs nyi shu lnga lon pa//rnyed nas mi yi skye gnas brtag/rnam par dpon nam gtsang ba yi//sa phyogs su ni rgyal bdag nyid//zhi ba zhi ba'i gnas yid 'ong //bshang gci 
181a7(rgyud): chu yis byugs nas ni//sangs rgyas byang chub rab ster ba'o//rtag tu mchod pa nyid bya'o//de bzhin gshegs pa mchod pa yi//phung po zhes bya sna tshogs skyes//rdo rje gsum po yi skye thabs mas//rgyal ba zhes ni bya bar bshad//kun mkhyen ye shes pa bzhin//len pa mi gsal 'khor la//de nyid 
181b1(rgyud): na chung yin par brtag/'jig rten pa rnams 'thob byed rgya//'gro ba'i don ni rab grub pa//gang zhig dngos med ces bshad pa//mtha' skyes bzhin du brtag pa yin//gtsang ba rnam dag sems yin te//rnam par dben pa dngos med pa//sa ni spyan zhes bya ba yin//sa phyogs rgyal ba 
181b2(rgyud): 'byung ba yin//zhi ba sna tshogs nges 'byung ba//zhi ba'i gnas ni myur la sogs//bshang gci yul dang dbang por grags//chu ni shes rab la sogs drug/'di dag gis gang byugs gyur pa//rdzogs longs spyod pa'i 'dod 'byung ba//de rtag rgyal ba'i chos bshad pa//dgongs pa sbyor ba 
181b3(rgyud): chos nyid do//mchod pa'i yan lag gnyis pa yi//bkod pa'i yan lag mtshan nyid bshad//'jig rten shin tu 'das spyi bo//'jig rten 'das pa'i snying ga ru//rkang ba'i bar dang lte ba dang //rked pa gsang ba 'jig rten pa//rgyal sras rnams kyi bkod pa ni//rigs ni lnga po bya ba yin//

181b5(rgyud): bzhin mchod rten ni//'od zer sna tshogs rnams kyis brgyan//ye shes rgya mtsho dus gsum pa//gnas de ru ni bsam bya ba//blo ldan ba spu'i bu ga las//ji lta ji ltar spro bar bya//rnal 'byor rnal 'byor mchog gzhol bas//mchod pa'i sprin ni des spro bya//gos dkar pad+mar yang dag 
181b6(rgyud): dgod//rnam lnga gcig la rnal 'byor ma//me yi sbyor ba bshad pa ni//de yang mi gsal zhi la gnas//sgros mar ut+pal nyid gyur pa//rlung ste sbyor ba lnga pa'o//'jug dang gnas dang skye ba dang //de bzhin ldang ba'i ming can no//de nyid rnam pa gsum byas pas//rdo rje gsum gyi dam 
181b7(rgyud): tshig gnas//de nas blo ldan gyis dbul ba//lha rnams kyi yang dngos med nyid//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa mchog/khyab bdag rnam snang chen po las//me tog dgongs pa thos nas ni//rdo rje can gyis thub chen zhus//me dang rlung dang dbang chen dang //chu ru yongs su brtags pa yin//re re 
182a1(rgyud): @#//'ang dang ro sbyor zhes bya//me tog de lnga gsum du bshad//bya rgod me tog za ba dang //bung bshad zhes zer zhing //me tog 'di yi dgongs pa ni//ma 'ongs skye bos mi shes so//ma rtogs pas ni de rnams kyi//dngos grub ring du gnas par 'gyur//'di ltar bdag ni skal min 
182a2(rgyud): zhes//sgra zhing de rnams bdag nyid smod//de ltar bdag nyid smad pas na//sangs rgyas thams cad smad par 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad rmad gyur pas//mi las 'das nas gzhan du 'gro//gtso bo sangs rgyas nang na min//phyi rol na min gnyis gar min//tshogs pa las kyang ma 
182a3(rgyud): yin te//rkyen gyis 'gro ba dag la gnas//de bzhin de bzhin gshegs sangs rgyas//lus la gsang sngags 'di nyid las//gsang sngags 'grub par dka' ba'i rgyus//des ni bdag dang de dag smod//de rnams rjes su gzung ba'i phyir//dgongs pa de ni bshad du gsol//
182a4(rgyud): rang gi ngo bo ma 'tshal na//mkhas pa dag ni nyams par 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan sna tshogs pas//gsang ba'i bdag po la gsungs pa//nyin dang mtshan mo'i dbye ba yis//me tog don ni bshad par bya//pad ut+pal nyin mor bshad//dzA ti mtshan mo yin zhes bshad//
182a5(rgyud): lnga yi bdag nyid gsum dang gcig/gnyis lnga gcig dang gsum du 'gyur//de yi thabs ni nyin mor bshad//mtshan mo shes rab ces bshad do//nyin dang mtshan mo'i dbyi ba yis//mjug tu 'byung ba nyid du 'gyur//de gnyis dbus kyi sbyor ba gang //dgongs pas 
182a6(rgyud): yongs su brtags pa yin//de ni sangs rgyas snying por 'gyur//de bas lha tshogs kyis de min//rnal 'byor pa dang sngags pa rnams//dgongs pa'i chos la rab brtson bya//dgongs pa byin rlabs rgyu yis ni//de rnams kyis ni 'grub par 'gyur//gang zhig rlung ni bcur grags 
182a7(rgyud): pa//de ni pad+ma ut+pal ming //de nyid ces grags rdo rje gsum//dzA ti gcig pu gsum du 'dod//ka Ni ka dang ma li ka/yu ka ti ka ra bI ra ni//go rims bzhin du spyan sogs te//sa la sogs par 'dir sbyar ro//dngos med go 'phang la bkod pas//lha rnams kyi ni skur sbyor bas//gcig tu 
182b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun mnyes pa'i//mchod pa chen por de 'gyur ro//lnga po'i bdag nyid yang dag bzhag/lnga pa bdag ni gnyis 'gyur bya//de nyid gsum sbyor 'khor lo ni//dpag tshad brgya yi dkyil 'khor ro//der ni mkhas pas rtag par ni//rigs rnams rab tu dgod par bya//pad+ma 'khor 
182b2(rgyud): lo rdo rje dang //de bzhin kun la rtag tu bsgom//dpag tshad bzhi po sbyor gcig pa//dpag tshad brgya byin de nyid gsum//byams pa la sogs srung zhing gyur//mdzes pa phung pos brgyan pa yin//gru bzhi de ni rigs su gyur//zhi ba sbyor ba gcig ming can//mchod rten ye shes 
182b3(rgyud): sems rang bzhin//gsal zhing rang bzhin dri ma med//rigs rnams mchod phyir de nyid ni//shes rab can gyis gnas bsgom mo//mngon shes lnga yi gnas 'dod rnams//de yi mchod pa rgya mtsho gsal//rin chen ye shes spro bdag nyid//gos ni bsdu ba zhes bya'o//mchod pa 
182b4(rgyud): mchog rnams de dag gis//byang chub 'dod pas mchod par bya//shes rab can gyis mchod mchog las//lha rnams rtag tu mnyes par bya//sbyor gcig de las yang dag byung //rin chen 'byung gnas na chung yin//dga' mgu yang dag ldan pa yi//dngos grub shin tu grub pa'i phyir//
182b5(rgyud): nga rgyal la sogs bdun zhes bya//rin chen thams cad dbul bar bya//chos dang rdzogs yongs spyod las byung //rin chen bdun gyi nor de ni//des bkang pa yi zhing 'di ni//shes rab thabs cig las byung ba'i//dngos grub 'dod pas sbyin pa'i phyir//zhing de nyin re dbul bar ni//
182b6(rgyud): nam mkha' mtha' yas rnal 'byor pa'i//sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin 'dir 'gyur//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor dbus su de//phyag rgya'i bdag por mnyam bzhag ste//'dod chags blo ldan sangs rgyas la//reg par yang dag sbyor ba dbul//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//
182b7(rgyud): bkod pa'i dkyil 'khor dam pa bsgom//bdag nyid de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku//yul dang dbang po sun 'byin pa//dngos med gcig par yang dag 'byung //yul dang dbang po zhes bya ba'i//bshang gci ji lta ji lta bar//de nyid mchod pa rnams kyis mchod//skal bzang zhing ni 
183a1(rgyud): @#//mdzes pa yi//byad med gzugs bzang rnyed pa dang //gsang sngags yi ge'i gnas 'byung bas//rnal 'byor ma de byin brlabs la//byas nas gnyis med sbyor gcig pu//rang gi lus kyi nang du gzhug/spyan sogs ji lta ji lta bar//byin gyis brlabs pa'i 
183a2(rgyud): gnas bkod de//mig bzang dag pa'i bya ba yi//rang byung khu ba blangs nas mchod//gang zhig de ltar rtogs pa de//blangs pas rdo rje 'chang bar 'gyur//sa la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor gang //spyan la sogs par rab brtags pa//de ni 'jug sogs sbyor ba yang //de ni khu 
183a3(rgyud): ba za bar 'gyur//'di ni sngags rnams thams cad kyi//sku gsung thugs kyi mchod pa ste//rdo rje ye shes can gsang gi//gsang sngags dngos grub byed pa'i mchog/rin chen kun gyi 'byung gnas mchog/dgongs pa lung bstan la gnas nas//gang zhig sems ni bzod gyur 
183a4(rgyud): pa//de ni ji bzhin nyid du'ang byed//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//rdo rje gsung gi gsang ba las//bshang gci'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i//le'u brgyad pa yin par bshad////de nas de bzhin gshegs mchog gtso//thams cad nam mkha' mi 
183a5(rgyud): 'gyur che//sangs rgyas dbang bskur spyod pa'i mchog/gzugs don dbang phyug dam pa yin//rdo rje gsum nyid longs spyod pa//rdo rje mi phyed gsum dkyil 'khor//sangs rgyas ye shes sbyor gcig pa'o//gsang ba nyams ni dga' bar bshad//nam mkha'i dbyings 
183a6(rgyud): kyi dbus gnas par//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor bsgrub par bya//rdo rje rtse gcig rdo rje yang //bsgom pa'i bdag nyid can gyis bsgom//'od 'phro mang po 'bar ba dang //'od zer sna lngas rab tu gang //dus gsum sangs rgyas yang dag byung //bsgoms la rdo rjes phye mar ma rlab//
183a7(rgyud): rdo rje gsum gcig longs spyod ni//rdo rjes phyir brlags med gyur pa'i//thugs kyi dngos grub byed bsam gtan//bsgoms na myur du thob par 'gyur//rdo rje mchog ces bya ba yi//rdo rje gsang ba 'di lta bu//rdo rjes rdo rje 'dzin pa ni//sems can thams cad bsad na ni//de ni 
183b1(rgyud): myur bar mi bskyod pa'i//rgyal ba'i sras su skye bar 'gyur//zhe sdang rigs chen de nyid kyis//zhing de 'gengs par byed par 'gyur//de nas dgongs pa chos gcig pu//gtso bo kun mkhyen thams cad reg/dam tshig de nyid gsungs pa yi//mgon la phyag na rdo rjes gsol//
183b2(rgyud): 'di ltar dngos med mchog gyur pa//khyod ni chos rnams ston mdzad pa//'jig rten pa dang 'gal tshig gis//mgon khyod ci phyir ston par mdzad//gang zhig de bzhin nyid dngos gcig/dus gsum 'byung ba'i sangs rgyas rnams//chos sku gcig pu las 'khrungs 
183b3(rgyud): pa||ji ltar rdo rjes phye mar brlab//de ni phye rab rlags gyur pa las//ji ltar yang dag sems 'grub 'gyur//chos sku gcig pu las/byung las//lhag pa zhes bya gyur nas su//dus gsum 'byung ba'i sad rgyas rnams//phye mar brlag par ji ltar nus//phyer brlag byed pas 
183b4(rgyud): dngos med pas//phye ma nyid kyang khyad par du//sangs rgyas sngags don rnal 'byor pa//nus pa bsam mi khyab ldan la//de 'dra bar ni phyer brlag par//sngags pa gzhan gyis mi nus so//sems can gyis ni sems can bzhin//sangs rgyas sangs rgyas phyer brlags pas//'di gnyis 
183b5(rgyud): khyad par med do zhes//ma 'ongs skye bo smra bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas gcig pu mthar thug pa//min zhes mi rnams nges smra zhing //gang zhig sangs rgyas kyis gsungs bzhin//rab tu spang bar byed par 'gyur//theg bzang bsgrubs na 'tshang rgya ba//dgongs pa'i bum pa bzang po mchog/de ni 
183b6(rgyud): bcom ldan myur sbyor bas//mi bskyod zhing du skye 'gyur ram//sems can 'khor bar 'khor gyur pa//de tshe zhe sdang che skye'am//snying rjes gsang bar bya'o zhes//sngags pa rnams ni de mi spong //'das tshe ston par gyur pa dang //da ltar rjes su ston mdzad dang //mnyes bya ma 'ongs sangs 
183b7(rgyud): rgyas rnams//sangs rgyas byang chub stsol ba'o//mi bskyod gzugs can rdo rje yis//de rnams ji ltar gzhom par bgyi//de nyid sna tshogs don ldan tshig/de rnams kyis ni ji ltar rtogs//gang zhig shes pas rgyal sras rnams//dgongs pa'i don la rtog med 
184a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur//ma 'ongs skye bo'i don du ni//de nyid bcom ldan bshad du gsol//de nas rdo rje 'dzin smra ba'i//'gro ma phan pa'i tshig gsan nas//de nas gtso bo rdo rje 'chang //khyab bdag gis ni slar gsungs pa//legs so bu khyod cher legs te//kun du bzang po khyod 
184a2(rgyud): legs so//dgongs pas bshad pa'i don du ni//khyod kyis nga la gang dris pa//gsungs don ji lta ji lta bar//de ni nga yis bshad par bya//phung sogs yan lag gang gyur pa//sangs rgyas sems can rab brtan zhing //sngags dang phyag rgya'i sbyor ba yang //dbye ba sna tshogs rab tu brtan//'khor 
184a3(rgyud): ba mi bskyod ming can te//de sngags rdo rje zhes bya ba//mi gsal rdo rje zhes bshad de//dngos med gcig pu rdo rje nyid//des bshig pa ni bsad pa ste//sangs rgyas sems can khyad par du//sna ni tshogs kyi dbang po yi//kha ni rang bzhin nyid du g.yo//rnal 'byor pa yi lag skur pa//
184a4(rgyud): 'gro ba rlung gi ming can no//mi bskyod rdo rje'i spyod pa nyid//lag tu rdo rje bzhag nas ni//de tshe myur la sogs bya ba//klu yi rang bzhin sna tshogs nyid//phung sogs brtags pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//sku gsung thugs las yang dag byung //de yi yan lag phung por bshad//de ni phyer 
184a5(rgyud): gyur bsad pa yin//'di ni zhe sdang rigs yid 'ong //mi bskyod ye shes rdo rje nyid//de bzhin skye ba dam pa yin//dngos grub chen po des thob 'gyur//de nas dpal ldan bde chen po//ye shes thar pa rab sgrub pa//rang bzhin dag gis gos med pa//byang chub spyod pa 
184a6(rgyud): rab bsgrubs pas//phyag na rdo rje 'od chen po//gsang ba gsum gyi gtso bo la//bos nas yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas//sgrub pa'i dam tshig de nyid gsungs//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//rdo rje dkyil 'khor dam pa la//rnam par snang mdzad bsgom bya zhing //
184a7(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad rnam par bsgom//rin chen kun gyi sbyor ba yis//rdo rje gzugs ni rnam par bsgom//rdo rje gsum mnyam sbyor ba yis//rdzas rnams thams cad dbrog par bya//shes dang dbang po ldog par 'gyur//rnam smin dngos po med pa yi//de nyid 'phrog pa'i rdo rje dang //
184b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad rab bsgrub pa'i//rin chen rdo rje mchog sbyor ba//rgyal ba'i sras su 'gyur ba dang //nor gyi rgya mtshos gang ba yi//yid bzhin nor bu 'dra bar 'gyur//'di ni gti mug rigs de nyid//dam tshig yin par yongs su bsgrags//rigs kun rgya mtsho 
184b2(rgyud): shes bya ste//de las thub pa'i skyes mchog 'gyur//de nas bdag po bcom ldan 'das//'dod chags thar par rab 'jug pa//gsang ba dag pa gnas med pa//dkyil 'khor mchog ni bka' stsal to//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//rin chen dkyil 'khor 
184b3(rgyud): bsgom par bya//dpag med chos ni rnam bsgoms te//sangs rgyas kun gyis rab tu dgang //bud med lta bur sbyor ba yang //ting nge 'dzin gyi sbyor ba yis//thams cad de ru bsgom par bya//'di ni rdo rje tshul mchog yin//dngos med bsam pa'i sbyor ba yis//gsal mi 
184b4(rgyud): gsal la yang dag sbyor//chos dang rdzogs longs spyod sbyor ba//gsang sngags kyis sprul dbang po gnyis//sbyor ba bzhi yi sbyor ba yis//dbang po gnyis ni rab sbyor ba'i//snyoms zhugs sbyor ba de yis ni//de dag thams cad nye bar spyod//sku gsum mi phyed sngags rnams kyi//
184b5(rgyud): 'di ni bsgom pa mchog de yin//de nyid dam tshig sbyor ba 'di//'dod chags rig gcig bsgom pa'o//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//bkod chags chen po'i tshul mchog ste//sngags pa rnams kyis nyin re zhing //rab tu 'bad pas bsgom par bya//de nas gsang dbang rdo rje 
184b6(rgyud): gsung //rdo rje sngags don sgrub pa po//bdag med ye shes las byung bas//gsung ni 'di skad bka' stsal to//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//don yod rdo rje rnam bsgoms te//sangs rgyas thams cad bsgom par bya//rdo rje chog gi brdzun smra ba//
184b7(rgyud): rgyal ba thams cad slu ba ste//'di ni sangs rgyas tham sa cad kyi//nam mkha'i gsung ni dri med pa'o//gang zhig brdzun du smra bshad pa//brdzun de nyid ni dam pa ste//dpa' bo gang zhig sgom byed pa//de ni rgyal ba'i mchog tu 'gyur//gsang ba'i ye shes dam pa ste//sangs rgyas dngos grub byed 
185a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i mchog/dam tshig 'gugs pa rag dgyur ba'i//rigs kyi dam tshig la 'jug pa'o//kun du kun nas 'bad pa yi//ji bzhin don du 'dra byas te//so sor rtog pa'i sbyor ba yis//de ni dngos grub rgya chen thob//de nas bcom ldan sna tshogs pa//mi phyed rdo rje gsum 
185a2(rgyud): rgyal ba dag/rdo rje'i dngos grub rab ston pas//gsung ni 'di skad bka' stsal to//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//der ni dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//sangs rgyas sku ru rab brtags pa'i//dam tshig de ni yang dag bshad//dkon mchog dpal ni rab bsgoms te//
185a3(rgyud): gzugs rnams kun gyis 'di gang //phung po la sogs grangs mnyam pa'i//sangs rgyas kun gyis thams cad du//rtsub pa'i ngag la sogs pa ni//sa rnams dman pa zhes bya ba'i//bsgoms na dngos grub thob 'gyur zhes//bcom ldan thub pas de skad gsungs//de bzhin 
185a4(rgyud): gshegs pa kun bkod pa//sna tshogs gzugs can 'od po che//bsam pas dpal ldan gcig pu nyid//dpag tu med par gyur pa yin//de nas gang zhig dam tshig sogs//chos dbyings rdo rje gsum nyid ni//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis//dam tshig rdo rje dpal la 
185a5(rgyud): sogs//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che//ngo mtshar rmad du gyur nas ni//tshig lam rdo rje zhes bya 'di//de ni de rnams rjod par byed//ci 'di mgon po bcom ldan 'das//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi bdag/gang zhig khams gsum las 'das shing //'jig rten 
185a6(rgyud): thams cad las 'das pa//de bzhin gshegs pa kum mchog dang //byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs chen gyi//'khor gyi dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//ngo mtshar che ba'i tshig gi lam//gtso bos yang dag rdo rje'i tshig/dgongs pas ci slad gang gsungs zhes//brjod kyis mi lang ba yi 
185a7(rgyud): yang //brjod mi lang ba rnams kyis gsol//de nas de bzhin gshegs kun gyis//brjod kyis mi lang pa yi yang //brjod kyis mi lang grangs kyis ni//ri rab rtul phran dang mnyam pa'i//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang //dam tshig rdo rje dpal sogs pa'i//byang chub sems dpa' 
185b1(rgyud): sems chen la//dngos med las byung gsal ba gsungs//dman dang smad pa'i 'du shes ni//rgyal ba'i sras rnams ma skyed cig/ci phyir zhe na de bzhin gshegs//'byung ba gang 'di spyod mchog yin//'di ltar sngags kyi spyod mchog 'di//rnam par dag yin rigs kyi bu//
185b2(rgyud): sangs rgyas mnyam med 'byung 'gyur 'di//gsang ba'i sngags don spyod pa nyid//'di ltar dper na rigs kyi bu//nam mkha' kun du song ba ste//chos rnams kun kyang der rtogs shing //rab dbye'i sbyor bar gyur pa nyid//'dod pa'i khams na mi gnas shing //de ni gzugs kyi khams 
185b3(rgyud): mi gnas//de bzhin gzugs med khams na'ang min//'byung ba bzhi la de mi gnas//rigs kyi bu chos thams cad kyang //de ltar don gyi dbang mkhyen nas//dgongs pa'i dbang gis rgyal ba rnams//de bzhin gshegs rnams dgongs nyid kyis//chos rnams ston par mdzad pa 
185b4(rgyud): yin//de bzhin du ni khyed rnams kyis//mdor na khong du chud par bya//yang dag byang chub don rnams kyis//nam mkha'i nges tshig sbyor bas ni//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sbyor byung ba'i//dam tshig rjes su rig par bya//'di ltar dper na rig kyi bu//gtsub shing gtsub stan bcas pa 
185b5(rgyud): dang //skyes bu'i lag pa bskyod pa yi//rkyen las du ba 'byung bar 'gyur//de nas me yang 'byung mod kyi//gtsub shing la sogs re re nas//'di ltar so sor brtags na go//me de gang na'ang mi gnas so//brag ca dang ni gzugs brnyan dang //sgyu ma dang ni smig rgyu dang //de bzhin chu 
185b6(rgyud): zla rmi lam ste//chos rnams thams cad de dang 'dra//mgon po pi bang sga lta bu//rkyen don las tsheg yang dag byung //de bzhin 'gro 'ong gnas pa rnams//cung zad tsam yang yod ma yin//gnas med gnas pa'i sbyor ba yis//gnas pa nam mkha'i mtshan nyid yin//des na 
185b7(rgyud): khyed kyis rdo rje yi//dam tshig rjes su rigs par bya//de ltar chos rnams dngos med ces//gsang ba'i de nyid don mkhas pas//rnam par smin pa ldog bya'i phyir//phung po de bzhin gshegs par shes//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rnams//ngo mtshar rmad du gyur nas

186b5(rgyud): bzhag nyid//de ltar pha rol phyin 'byung ba'i//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga dang ldan//'gro ba'i don gyi bya ba yang //'dod pa lnga po 'di yis mdzad//'di rnams kyis ni mgon po 'di//mi bskyod la sogs rgyal ba rnams//rol cing dgyes par mdzad pa dang //
186b6(rgyud): 'gro ba'i don kyang mdzad gyur cing //'jig rten pa dang 'gal bya rnams//de yang de bzhin mdzad gyur pa//dag pa rdo rje'i tshig mchog 'di//de rnams kyis ni rtogs par 'gyur//phyag byar 'os pa'i rig pa ni//nges par ldan pa nges par 'dod//yod med la sogs

201b3(rgyud): 'jam pa'i rdo rje bdag po ste//'di dag 'khor du gyur pa yin//'jig rten pa yi rang ngo bo//phyi yi ri rab brtag pa yin//dam tshig rdo rje zhes bya'o//'khor lo'i ting nge 'dzin ces bya//'khor lo dngos grub rnyed sla ba//skad cig sbyor

201b5(rgyud): ni//gsum 'gyur gnas su 'gyur ba yin//khams gsum gyi ni rdo rje che//drug po yis ni phyag byas 'gyur//rdo rje dam tshig ces bya ba'i//ting 'dzin 'di ni yang dag bshad//pad+ma chen po 'dab brgyad pa//rang sngags rdo rje las byung 
201b6(rgyud): ba//chos rnams kun gyi gnas bsgoms na//rdo rje chos dang 'dra bar 'gyur//sum cu rtsa drug gnas sbyor ba'i//ri rab rdul phran grangs snyed kyi//sangs rgyas rdo rje mchog gi ni//dkyil 'khor la bdag bdag nyid 'jog/gos med mnyam nyid ye shes 
201b7(rgyud): kyi//rdo rje pad+mo grub bcas sbyin//so sor rtog pa zhes bya ba'i//ting nge 'dzin ni yang dag bshad//shin tu grangs med bskal par ni//grangs kyi ming gis gnas par ni//gnas shing sgom par byed pa yin//de yi ye shes lngar ldan pa//sangs

202b2(rgyud): bsgoms te//ye shes rdo rje dag gis bya//'dzam bu'i chu bo'i gser mdog gam//nyi ma 'char ka'i 'od 'dra pa'i//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//rdo rje mi phyed rab bsgoms te//lag tu rdo rje bsgoms na ni//rdo rje'i rig pa 'dzin par

202b4(rgyud): rin chen rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur///rin chen mchog gi bsgrub pa 'di///rin chen sku yi sbyor ba las///rin chen rigs kyi mchog bsgom pa //ye shes rdo rje dag gisabya//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis dbus gnas par//tshe dpag med pa rab bsgoms la//

202b7(rgyud): bsgrub bya rdo rje'i dbye ba yis//mdung thung rdo rje lcags kyu sogs//sku gsung thugs kyi bsgrub pa yis//bsam gtan nyid kyis bsgrub par 'gyur//dam tshig chen po 'byung ba yi//dam tshig dngos grub ces bya ba'i//mi phyed rdo rje gsum dbang phyug/

203a7(rgyud): pad+ma la sogs bya//me long lta bu'i sngags pas ni//sangs rgyas sku ni rnam par bsgom//lag tu sku dang gsung thugs kyi//lcags kyu la sogs rab tu bsgom//de ni sbyor ba 'di nyid kyis//de nyid las ni lha rnams dgod//de ni de ma 
203b1(rgyud): thag tu yang //dngos grub kun ster las byed 'gyur//sku dang gsung thugs thams cad ni//mtshan nyid gcig par bsgom par bya//lce yi rdo rje sbyor ba yis//gsung gi rdo rje de mnyam bsgom//brtul zhugs can gyis phyi nang gnyis//
203b2(rgyud): gsang ba'i rdo rje mchog mchod do//dngos med dam tshig sbyor ba yis//ji lta ji ltar rab bsgrub pa//dam tshig gsum gyi rnal 'byor ba'i//de ni yang dag yin par bshad//gsang ba'i snying po bsdus pa yi//dngos grub kun gyi mchog tu 'dod//
203b3(rgyud): bcom ldan dam tshig chen po yi//rnal 'byor bas ni de skad gsungs//de nas phyag na rdo rje ni//go byed gsang ba yang dag gsungs//gang zhig shes na rnal 'byor ba'i//mnyam gzhag mchog ni thob 'gyur ba'i//gsang dbang de nyid dam pa 
203b4(rgyud): yi//de ni de yis yang dag bshad//rnal 'byor chen po gcig bdag nyid//nam mkha' dang ni mnyam pa'i gnas//byas na dam tshig yang dag pa//sha chen la ni yang dag sbyar//dngos med pa yi sha chen gyi//dam tshig mchog

244a1(rgyud): @#//nam mkha' la//'od gsal bzang po yang dag gnas//sangs rgyas rnams kyi shes pa bcu//de lta bu nyid bshad par bya//'pho ba bcu ni sa bcu ste//rim pa yi ni rnam par shes//ma ni shes rab pha rol phyin//b+ha ga phyag rgya chen por brjod//
